sanikuṇuṭakuḻūra ñā. tēvanēyap pāvāṇar

patippuri
tamiḻukkum tamiḻarukkum vaḷamum valimiyum cērkkak
kūṭiya paḻantamiḻ nūlkaḷiyellām tēṭiyeṭuttut tamiḻ kūṟum ulakiṟku vaḻaṅkum tolinōkkup pārviyuṭaṉ  tamiḻmaṇ patippakam toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ vāyilāka moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ nūṟṟāṇṭu niṉivāka avar eḻutiya nūlkaḷ aṉittiyum cērttu avaruṭiya maṟivukkup piṟaku 2000-ttilum, palvēṟu itaḻkaḷilum, malarkaḷilum veḷivantu, nūl vaṭivam peṟāta pāvāṇariṉ ariya kaṭṭurikaḷi ellām tokuttu 2001- lum orucēra veḷiyiṭṭu ulakeṅkum uḷḷa tamiḻarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkiṉōm.  pāvāṇar vaḻi nili aṟiñarkaḷāṉa mutumuṉivar 
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum, maruttuvar ku.pūṅkāvaṉam avarkaḷum ivvariya kaṭṭurip putiyalkaḷ nūl vaṭivam peṟuvataṟku mikavum uṟutuṇiyāka iruntaṉar. ipperumakkaḷi naṉṟiyuṇarvuṭaṉ niṉivukūrkiṟēṉ.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tēvanēyar oppaṟṟa taṉit
tiṟamiyuṭivar eṉṟu maṟimaliyaṭikaḷum, nāvalan tīvukku  nantamiḻē tāyeṉṟu pāvēntarum, tamiḻukkum tamiḻ
nāṭṭukkum naṟṟoṇṭu āṟṟiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pēraṟiñar aṇṇā avarkaḷum, veṭṭa veṭṭak kiṭikkum taṅkac
curaṅkam pōṉṟavar eṉṟu tamiḻiṟiñar irācamāṇikkaṉārum, tamiḻakam moḻittuṟiyilē pāvāṇar pōṉṟa oru aṟiñari iṉṉum peṟṟut taravilli eṉṟu paṉmoḻip pulavar
appātturiyarum, kuṟimatiyar tēkkivitta kariyiruḷi nīkka vanta maṟimaliyār vaḻivanta niṟimaliyār pāvāṇar eṉṟu mēṉāḷ pēravittalivar tamiḻkuṭimakaṉ avarkaḷum, tamiḻar yār? etirikaḷ yār? eṉṟu āyntu aṟintu kāṭṭiyavar pāvāṇar eṉṟu pērāciriyar iḷavaracu avarkaḷum,  ōrāyiram āṇṭukaḷ ōyntu kiṭantapiṉ vārātu pōla vanta māmaṇi pāvāṇar eṉṟu mutumuṉivar iḷaṅkumaraṉār avarkaḷum kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷa  perumikaḷukkuriya pēraṟiñariṉ nūlkaḷi mīḷpatippaka veḷiyiṭuvatil perumi koḷkiṟēṉ.
pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ attaṉiyum tamiḻmoḻikku ēṟṟam urippaṉa. tamiḻi āriya iruḷiṉiṉṟu mīṭṭuk kāppaṉa. vīḻntu
paṭṭa tamiḻaṉukku viḻippūṭṭa vallaṉa. āyiramāyiram āṇṭukaḷāka aṭimippaṭṭukkiṭakkum tamiḻmoḻiyi mīṭkavallatu eṉṟu  pāvalarēṟu peruñcittiraṉār avarkaḷiṉ kūṟṟi ik kaḷañciya veḷiyīṭṭil pativu ceyvatu ematu kaṭamiyākum.
pāvāṇarit tūkkip piṭittāltāṉ tamiḻiṉam urup
paṭamuṭiyum - uyaramuṭiyum. pāvāṇar koḷkikaḷ tamiḻar uḷḷamellām nilittu niṟpataṟkum, pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ tamiḻar illamelām iṭam peṟuvataṟkum muṉ kuṟippiṭṭa 2000 - 2001 kālakkaṭṭattil veḷiyiṭappaṭṭa pāvāṇariṉ aṟivuk karuvūlaṅkaḷiyellām oṭṭu mottamāka cērttu orē vīccil pāvāṇar tamiḻk kaḷañciyam eṉum talippil  em aṟakkaṭṭaḷi veḷiyiṭukiṟatu.
maṟikkappeṟṟa māperum varalāṟṟiyum, citikkap peṟṟa oppuyarvuyaṟṟa moḻiyiyum koṇṭa tamiḻiṉattiṉ muṉṉēṟṟam karuti ivvaruntamiḻ putiyalkaḷi tamiḻ kūṟum nallulakiṟku  vaḻaṅka muṉ vantuḷḷōm. tamiḻ moḻiyi mūccākavum, pēccākavum, ceyalākavum koṇṭu orunāḷiṉ muḻuppoḻutum tamiḻākavē vāḻnta cempulaccemmal,  taṉittamiḻk katiravaṉ moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷi vāṅkip payaṉ koḷvīr.
iḷamiyilēyē potuttoṇṭilum, taṉittamiḻ iyakkat toṇṭilum nāṉ īṭupāṭu koḷvataṟkuk kāraṇamāka iruntavarkaḷ perumpulavar nakkīraṉ avarkaḷum, avartam iḷaval pulavar cittiravēlaṉ avarkaḷum āvar. ivarkaḷi ivveḷiyiṭṭiṉ vāyilāka naṉṟiyuṇarvōṭu niṉivu kūṟukiṟēṉ.  tanti periyāriṉ taṉmatippu iyakkak koḷki
kaḷālum, moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇariṉ taṉittamiḻ iyakkak koḷkikaḷālum īrkkappaṭṭavaṉ. attaku piṉpulattōṭu patippuppaṇiyil eṉ kālaṭic cuvaṭukaḷip patittu varukiṟēṉ.
kō. iḷavaḻakaṉ,
patippāḷar.

vāṉmaḻi vaḷaccurappu
āḻattuḷ āḻamāy akaḻntu taṅkam ēṉ eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu? āḻkaṭaluḷ ceṉṟu, uyirip paṇayam vittu muttuk kuḷikkap paṭuvatu ēṉ? pavaḻap pāḷaṅkaḷi eṭuppatu ēṉ? vayiram mutalām maṇikkulaṅkaḷi iṭiyaṟāmal tēṭit tēṭi eṭuppatu ēṉ?
aṇikalap poruḷkaḷ eṉpataṟku maṭṭumā? vīṭṭiṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi nāṭṭukkum īṭṭutaṟku ariya vippu vaḷamāka iruntu, nāṭṭiṉ poruḷiyal mēmpāṭṭukku aṭimaṇi
yākavum ivi iruppatāl tāṉē!
ivaṟṟiṉum mēlām vippu vaḷamum uṇṭō? uṇṭu! uṇṭu! avi cāṉṟōr nūlkaḷ!
kālam kālamāka aḷḷik koṇṭālum vaṟṟā vaḷamāyt tikaḻum aṟivuk karuvūlamām nūlkaḷ!
oṉṟik kaṟṟāl oru nūṟāy ōrāyiramāyp palkip perukat takka peṟaṟkariya tāy nūlkaḷām pēṟaminta nūlkaḷ! cēynūlkaḷi īṉavalla ceḻu nūlkaḷ!
nuṇ māṇ nuḻipulat tāy nūlkaḷāy iruppiṉum! avaṟṟit tāṅkiya tāḷkaḷ, ettakiya pēṇutal uṭiyavi eṉiṉum kāla veḷḷattil aḻiyak kūṭiyavi tāmē!
kalleḻuttē, katir veppālum kaṭaluppālum kāttup pēṇum kaṭappāṭillār kippaṭalālum aḻintu paṭṭami kaṇkūṭu tāṉē!
ēṭṭu varivukaḷ, nīrē neruppē nīṭitta kālamē puṟṟē pōṟṟā maṭamiyē eṉpavaṟṟāl aḻintu paṭṭamikku aḷavu tāṉum uṇṭō? makkaḷukku māṇap periya payaṉām nūlkaḷ, kaṟkum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum, piṟantu perukivarum makkaḷ perukkattiṟkum takka vakiyil avarkaḷukkup pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭiyar vitta paḻantēṭṭeṉṉa annūlkaḷ, mīḷamīḷat taṭṭillāmal kiṭikkac ceyya vēṇṭuvatu nūṟpatippar tavirāk kaṭamiyallavō!
akkaṭamiyi avarkaḷ kālantōṟum kaṭappāṭākak koṇṭu ceyya, āḷum aracum vāḻum aṟivarum arunnuṇiyātal tāṉē kaṭṭāyat tēvi! ivvāṟu patipparum aṟivarum āḷnarum tattam mūccuk kaṭaṉāka, nūṟkoṭi puritalukku mēṟkoṭi illiyām!
moḻiñāyiṟu pāvāṇar nūlkaḷ oṉṟā iraṇṭā? namakku eṭṭātavi pōka eṭṭiya nūlkaḷ 32. kaṭṭuri poḻivu mutalām vakiyāl kiṭṭiya tiraṭṭu nūlkaḷ 12. iṉṉum eṭṭāta tokuppum aṅkoṉṟum iṅkoṉṟum vara vāyppuṇṭu!
kaṭita varivu pā varivu eṉa mēlum perukavum vāyppuṇṭu!
ivaṟṟi ellām āyiram acciṭṭu avvaḷavil niṉṟu viṭṭāl, tēṭuvārkkut tēṭum poḻutil vāykkum tiruvākat tikaḻak kūṭumō?
ātalāl, cēy nūlkaḷukku mūlamām tāynūlkaḷ, kālam tōṟum vāṉmaḻi vaḷac curappāka veḷippaṭa vēṇṭum kaṭṭāyam uṇarntu kaṭami puriyum vīṟuṭiyār vēṇṭum! mika vēṇṭum!
ittakiya viḻumiya vīṟuṭiyar - iṉamāṉac cemmal - tamiḻp pōrāḷi - tiruttaku kō. iḷavaḻakaṉār, tām muntu muḻutuṟak koṇarnta pāvāṇar paṭippukaḷi mīḷamīḷat tamiḻulāk koḷḷa vikkum muṉ muyaṟciyāy, im mīḷpatippi vaḻaṅkukiṟār! tamiḻulakam payaṉ koḷvatāka! payaṉ ceyvatāka!
vāḻiya nalaṉē! vāḻiya nilaṉē!
iṉpa aṉpuṭaṉ
irā. iḷaṅkumaraṉ.


cāṉṟitaḻ
paṇṭita ñā. tēvanēyaṉār, pi.ō.el. potuvāka moḻinūl āyvu muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟit tamiḻccol ārāyccipaṟṟi eḻutiya nūlkaḷ tamiḻmoḻiyiṉ nīṇṭakālat tēviyiṉi niṟivu ceytaṉa. tammuṭiya iḷantik kālattilē talimik kaṇkāṇiyār tavattiru tirañcu (Trench) eḻutiya collārāycci, pērāciriyar mākkacu mullar eḻutiya moḻiyaṟiviyal, pērāciriyar cāycu eḻutiya oppiyal moḻinūl mutaliya āṅkiya nūlkaḷi yāmē peruviruppuṭaṉ paṭittukkoṇṭirukkumpōtu anta muṟiyil tamiḻc coṟkaḷi ārāya vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpiṉēm. tamiḻp pēraṟiñarkaṭkup pulappaṭāmal maṟintu kiṭanta virivākavum viyappākavumuḷḷa tamiḻmoḻiyaṟivup parappu, paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel eḻutiya tirāviṭa moḻi
kaḷiṉ oppiyal āyvu nūlāl pulappaṭalāyiṟṟu. eṉiṉum paṇṭārakar kālṭuvel, aṟiyappaṭāta vaṭṭārattil ceyta
toru muyaṟciyātalāl tamiḻccoṟkaḷi yellām viṭāmal niṟivāka eṭuttārāyntuḷḷaṉar eṉṟu etirpārppataṟ
killi. ituvē moḻiyiyali ārāya vēṇṭu meṉṟu emmit tūṇṭiyatu. eṉavē `ñāṉacākaram (aṟivukkaṭal) eṉṉum emmuṭiya itaḻiṉ mutal tokutiyil at tuṟiyil oṉṟiraṇṭu kaṭṭurikaḷi eḻuti veḷiyiṭṭēm. āṉāl appōtu camayam, meypporuḷiyal, ilakkiya varalāṟu ākiya tuṟikaḷil emmuṭiya moḻiyārāyccit tuṟiyil toṭarntu īṭupaṭak kūṭavilli. āyiṉum takutiyuṭiya aṟiñar yārāvatu it tuṟiyil ārāyvataṟku muṉvarakkūṭumā eṉṟu etirpārttuk koṇṭiruntēm. appōtu yāḻppāṇam tiruttanti ñāṉappirakācartām ekipatiya moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūli emakku aṉuppivittār.  atu ōraḷavu emakku maṉaniṟivu aḷittatu. eṉiṉum moḻiyiyal ārāyccit
tuṟi mikavum virivum āḻamu muṭiyatātalāl paḻantamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷik kaṟṟu avaṟṟik koṇṭu naṉku purintukoḷḷum vakiyil mēlum moḻiyiyal ārāycci nūlkaḷ veḷivarutal vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutiṉēm. anta nērattil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār yām etirpārttati ēṟattāḻa muṟṟum niṟivēṟṟiyatu kaṇṭu perumakiḻvuṟṟēm. at tuṟiyil avar mikavum uḻippeṭuttu ārāyntu eḻutiyiruppavaṟṟit tamiḻ aṟiñarkaḷ naṉṟāka nampalām.
collārāyccit tuṟiyil tiru. tēvanēyaṉār oppaṟṟa taṉit tiṟamiyuṭiyavar eṉṟum, avarukku oppāka iruppavar arumiyākum eṉṟum yām uṇmiyākavē karutukiṉṟēm.
mēlum tiru. tēvanēyaṉār patiyac collum āciriyarum iṉpuṟuttum coṟpoḻivāḷarumāvar. pala tamiḻk kaḻaka āṇṭu viḻākkaḷil ematu talimiyiṉkīḻ avar coṟ
poḻivukaḷ āṟṟiyuḷḷār. avi kēṭṭārp piṇikkun takiyavāy amintu aviyōrik kiḷarcciyuṟac ceytu makiḻccik kaṭalil āḻttiṉa. mt® tUªâíiH¤J MuhŒ¢á brŒJtU« m¿P® Mjyhš mtiu¥ gÂÆš mk®¤J« vªj Ãiya¤J¡F« mtuhš ngU« òfG« »il¡f¥

- maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ
*  maṟimaliyaṭikaḷār āṅkilattil eḻutiya cāṉṟitaḻiṉ tamiḻākkam.

mukavuri
``	ōṅka liṭivan tuyarntōr toḻaviḷaṅki
ēṅkolinīr ñālat tiruḷakaṟṟum - āṅkavaṟṟuḷ
miṉṉēr taṉiyāḻi veṅkatiroṉ ṟēṉiyatu
taṉṉē rilāta tamiḻ.''	
(taṇṭi. urimēṟkōḷ)
eṉṟu pāṭiṉār oru paḻampulavar. ulakil mutaṉmutal makkaṭku eṉṟu pāṭiṉār oru paḻampulavar. ulakil tīntamiḻi, taṉṉē oḷḷiya nūlkaḷāl teḷḷiya aṟivu pukaṭṭiya tīntamiḻi, taṉṉērilāta moḻiyeṉṟu pukaḻntatu takkatē. āyiṉ, āriyam avvuṇmiyi muṟṟum maṟittu, tamiḻi aṭimutal muṭivari tarukkap poruḷākki vittiruppatāl, appōrāṭṭattālum tamiḻ taṉṉē rilātatākiṉṟatu. ittakiya etirppunili vēṟem moḻikkumilli.
tamiḻ neṭuṅkaṇakkamippu vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamāliyit taḻuviyateṉṟum, tamiḻ varivaṭivukaḷ pirāmiyeḻuttiṉiṉṟu tirintavi yeṉṟum;
tamiḻ aṭippaṭic coṟkaḷellām vaṭacol
leṉṟum; 
tolkāppiyam pirāticākkiyaṅkaḷiyum pāṇiṉīyattiyum camaṟkiruta nāṭaka nūlkaḷiyum alaṅkāra cāttirattiyum piṟavaṟṟiyum piṉpaṟṟiyeḻunta teṉṟum, aintiṇi yeṉṉum pakuppu vētattiṟ `pañcajaṉa' eṉṟu collap
paṭṭatē yeṉṟum;
tirukkuṟaḷ, aṟattuppālil maṉutaruma cāttirattiyum kāmāntakar nīticārattiyum, poruṭ pāliṟ kuṭiliyar artta cāttirattiyum, kāmattup pālil vāṟcāyaṉar kāmacūttirattiyum, taḻuviya teṉṟum; srīvallapa eṉpavarē tiruvaḷḷuvar eṉṟum, muppāl eṉpatu tirivarka (pa) eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppeṉṟum;
tamiḻar vaṇaṅkun teṟvaṅkaḷellām āriyat teyvaṅkaḷeṉṟum, uruttiraṉi (rutraṉi)c civaṉ eṉṟum, viṣṇuvit tirumāl eṉṟum, cuppiramaṇiyaṉi murukaṉ eṉṟum tamiḻar moḻi peyarttuk koṇṭaṉar allatu peyar māṟṟik koṇṭaṉar eṉṟum;
tēvāra tiruvācaka nālāyirat teyvap paṉuvalkal vētapporuḷiyē viḷakka vantaṉa eṉṟum, periya
purāṇamum tiruviḷiyāṭaṟpurāṇamum meykaṇṭāṉ nūlum vaṭanūlkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarp peṉṟum;
tirāviṭar eṉpār, vētavoḻukkattiṉiṉṟu vaḻuvi
yiḻinta cattiriyaruḷ orupirivārē yeṉṟum, pāratam allatu camputtīvam eṉṉum āriya tēcattiṉ teṉkōṭi maṇṭilamē tirāviṭam eṉṟum, tamiḻmoḻi āriyattiripāṉa oruvakip pirākirutam eṉṟum;
iṅṅaṉam āṉikoṉṟāṉ eṉṉum malippāmpu ōr yāṉi muḻuvatiyum viḻuṅkiṉāṟ pōṉṟu, iṉam moḻi ilakkiyam nākarikam paṇpāṭu eṉṉum aiṅkūṟaminta tamiḻam muḻuvatiyum āriyam viḻuṅkak kavvi viṭṭatu. cuntaram piḷḷi, maṟimaliyaṭikaḷ mutaliyōr tamiḻip pēraḷavu mīṭṭiruppiṉum, mūla viyāpuriyārum vaḻinili viyāpuriyārum ammīṭpiṉ payaṉip perituṅ keṭuttuviṭṭaṉar. akkēṭṭi nīkkavum, iṉimēl attakiya kēṭu eṉṟēṉum nērātirukkavum, varalāṟu, māntaṉūl (Anthropology), moḻinūl ākiya munnūlaṭip
paṭiyil innūl eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
uṇmiyāṉa tamiḻ allatu tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟu ōr uṟaḻcci nūlākavē yirukka muṭiyum eṉpati,
``	maṟimutaṟ kiḷanta vāyāṉ matimukiḻ muṭitta vēṇi
iṟivartam peyari nāṭṭi yilakkaṇañ ceyyap peṟṟē
aṟikaṭal varippiṟpāṭi yaṉittumveṉ ṟāriyattō
ṭutaḻtaru tamiḻttey vatti yuṇṇiṉin tēttal ceyvām.''
eṉṉum pāṭṭum kuṟippāka terivikkum.

tamiḻilakkiya varalāṟu - 1








muṉṉuri
1. maṟinta kumarikkaṇṭam (The Lost Lemuria)
nam ñālattiṉ mēṟparappil, iṉṟu kāynta nilamā yiruppatu, oru kālattiṟ kaṭalaṭi nilamāyum iṉṟu kaṭalaṭi nilamāyiruppatu oru kālattiṟ kāynta nilamāyum, iruntateṉṟu aṟiviyalāṟ potuvāka oppukkoḷḷap paṭṭuḷḷatu. nilanūlār cilaviṭaṅkaḷil, inta nila mūḻkalkaḷum eḻuccikaḷum nērntuḷḷa nilapparappiṉ tiṭṭavaṭṭamāṉa pakutikaḷik kuṟittuk kāṭṭak kūṭiyavarāy iruntirukkiṉṟaṉar. maṟinta aṟṟilāṇṭicuk kaṇṭavuṇmi ituvari aṟiviyalulakiṟ ciṟṟaḷavē oppam peṟṟiruppiṉum, ilemuriyā eṉṟu peyariṭappaṭṭuḷḷa oru paranta teṉ kaṇṭam varalāṟṟiṟku muntiyatoru kālattil iruntatu paṟṟi aṟiviyalāriṉ potuk karuttoṟṟumi nīṇṭa kālamāka iruntu varukiṉṟatu. 1
2. kumarikkaṇṭamē māntaṉ piṟantakam (tōrā. ki.mu. 1,00,000)
intumāvāri muṉpu oru kaṇṭamāyiruntatu. atu cantāt tīvukaḷiliruntu āciyāviṉ teṉkari vaḻiyē āppirikkāviṉ kiḻakkukkarivari parantiruntatu. ip paḻaṅkālak kaṇṭattiṟku, atil vāḻntirunta māntaṉ pōṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷpaṟṟi, kiḷēṟṟar (Sclater) eṉṉum āṅkilēyar ilemuriyā (Lemuria) eṉṟu peyariṭṭirukkiṉṟār.  atōṭu, atu māntaṉ piṟantakamā yiruntirukkak kūṭumeṉpatu paṟṟi, mikunta mutaṉmi vāyntatākum. 2
ilemuriyarē varalāṟṟiṟkut terinta mutal tiṭṭa vaṭṭamāṉa māntaṉ vakuppāreṉpatiyum, ilemuriyāk kaṇṭamē māntaṉ nākarikattiṉ uṇmiyāṉa piṟappiṭam
1. The Lost Lemuria by Scott Elliot., p. 1
2. History of Creation by Earnest Haeckel, Vol. I, p.361
eṉpatiyum, karutip pārkkumpōtu, ilemuriyāviṉiṉṟum ataṉ makkaḷiṉiṉṟumē māntaṉiṉ iṟṟi vakuppiṉaṅ kaḷellām tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉa eṉṉum iṉpuṟuttum uṇmiyik kāṇkiṟōm. 3
iṉṟuḷḷa aiṅkaṇṭaṅkaḷuḷ, āttirēliyāvēṉum amerikkāvēṉum airōppāvēṉum (māntaṉiṉ) im muntaka mākavō `paratīcu eṉṟu collappaṭum viṇṇulakamākavō mānta iṉattiṉ piṟantakamākavō iruntirukka muṭiyātu. perumpāṟ cūḻnilikaḷ teṉṉintiyāviyē nāṭumiṭamākak kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟuḷḷa kaṇṭaṅkaḷuḷ, teṉṉāciyā vallātu ivvakiyiṟ karutak kūṭiya orē kaṇṭam āppirik kāvē. āyiṉ, māntaṉiṉ muntakam, kiḻakkil appāli yintiyāvum cantāt tīvukaḷum variyum, mēṟkil maṭakācukkarum āppirikkāviṉ teṉkiḻakkuk kariyum variyum, āciyāviṉ teṉkari yoṭṭi, atu iṉṟu uḷḷa aḷavu (orukāl ataṉoṭu oṉṟāka iṇintu) parantiruntatum, iṉṟu intumāvāriyiṉkīḻ mūḻkik kiṭappatumāṉa oru kaṇṭamā yiruntateṉṟu uṉṉuvikkum cūḻnilikaḷ (ciṟappāka vaṭṭāra viyal uṇmikaḷ) pala vuḷḷaṉa. vilaṅkukaḷum nilit tiṇiyum paṟṟiya pala ñālanūluṇmikaḷ, attakiya teṉṉintiyak kaṇṭattiṉ muṉṉuṇmiyip peritum nampat takka tākkukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu, muṉpē kūṟiyuḷḷōm...... inta ilemuriyā māntaṉiṉ muntakamā yiruntateṉṟu koḷvataṉāl, makkaḷiṉaṅ kaḷiṉ nāṭupeyarvā lēṟpaṭṭa ñālaviyaṟ pātīṭṭiṉ viḷakkattiṟku mikat tuṇi ceykiṉṟōm. 4
māntaṉ tōṉṟi vāḻtaṟkum paṭippaṭiyāka nākarika vaḷarcciyaṭitaṟkum ēṟṟa nāṉila amippu, teṉṉāṭṭi lēyē iṉṟum ciṟappākavuḷḷatu.
iyaṟki allatu anākarika māntaṉ, iyaṟkiyāka viḷiyum kāykaṉi kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum vēṭṭiyāṭip peṟum vilaṅku paṟavi yiṟicciyiyum, uṇṭu vāḻvataṟkēṟṟa 
iṭam, kuṟiñci eṉṉum malinilam; ataṟkaṭuttapaṭiyāka, 
āṭumāṭu kaḷic ciṟappāka vaḷarttum vāṉa vārip payirkaḷi viḷittum vāḻvataṟku ēṟṟa iṭam, pulveḷiyuṅ kuṟuṅkāṭu muḷḷa mulli nilam; ataṟkaṭuttapaṭiyāka, naṉceyp payir 
3. Lemuria - The Lost Continent of the Pacific, p.181
4. History of Creation, Vol. II, pp.125-6
kaḷiyum puṉceyp payirkaḷiyum naṉṟāka viḷivittu, niliyāka vāḻntu, nāḷaṭiviṟ ciṟṟūr pērūrum, pērūr mūtūrum āki, kittoḻilum vāṇikamum vaḷarntu, kalviyum aracum nakarum nakaramum tōṉṟi, makkaḷ nākarikam aṭitaṟkēṟṟa iṭam, nilavaḷamum nīrvaḷamum mikka marutanilam; ataṟkaṭutta paṭiyāka, palavaki marakkalaṅkaḷ puṉintu kaṭal kaṭantu nīrvāṇikañ ceytu palvakip paṇṭaṅ koṇarntu nāṭṭi vaḷampaṭuttuvataṟku ēṟṟa iṭam, kaṭal cārnta neytalnilam. innālvaki nilamum aṭuttaṭut tiruntatum iṉṟum iruppatum, muṟiyē, muḻukippōṉa kumarik kaṇṭamum iṟṟit tamiḻakamumē.
pāli yeṉpatu, mulliyum ciṟumalik kuṟiñciyum mutu vēṉiṟ kālattil nīrnilikaḷellām vaṟṟi vaṟaṇṭu nilamuñ cuṭum nili. piṉṉar maḻikkālattiṟ pālinilam taḷirttum nīr nirampiyum mīṇṭum mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyumāka māṟiviṭum. ātalāl, aintiṇikaḷuṭ pālikku niliyāṉa nilamilli. ataṉāl, ñālatti nāṉilam eṉṟaṉar.
iṟṟit tamiḻnāṭṭiṟ pōṉṟē, paṇṭit tamiḻakamākiya kumarināṭṭilum, mēl kōṭiyilēyē oru paṉmaliyaṭukkattup perumalit toṭariruntatu. atu kumari yeṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarāṟ kumarimali yeṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉ teṉkōṭiyiṟ paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉum kaṅki pōlum māpēriyāṟum, vaṭa kōṭiyiṟ kumariyeṉṉum kāviri pōlum pēriyāṟum, tōṉṟik kiḻakkumukamāyp pāyntōṭiṉa. nilam mēṟkil uyarntum kiḻakkil tāḻntum iruntataṉāl kuṭatici mēltici (mēl-mēṟku) yeṉṟum, kuṇatici kīḻttici yeṉṟum (kīḻ-kīḻkku-kiḻakku), peyar peṟṟaṉa. iṟṟit tamiḻakattilum oruvaṉ mēṟṟiciyiṉiṉṟu kīḻttici variṉ, kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal eṉṉum nālvaki nilamum muṟiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tāḻntu aṭuttaṭuttirukkak kāṇpāṉ. naṇṇilakkaṭal 
(Mediterranean Sea) oru kālattil āciyāvi ūṭaṟuttuc ceṉṟu amiti māvāriyiṟ (Pacific Ocean) kalantatākat terivatāl, viviliyat toṭakkap pottakattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷavāṟu, ētēṉ tōṭṭam eṉṉum māntaṉ piṟantakam, mecop pottāmiyā nāṭṭil iruntirukka muṭiyātu. ēṉeṉiṉ, an nilappakuti paṇṭu kaṭalāyiruntirukkum.
nilanūliyal ūḻikaḷuḷ (Geological Eras) iṟutiyatāṉa puttiyaluyir ūḻiyic (Cainozoic Era) cērnta nāṉkām maṇṭalattuk (Quarternary Period) kaḻipallaṇmik (Pleistocene) kālappirivil ki.mu. 4,75,000 mutal ki. mu. 50,000 vari nāṉmuṟi paṉikkaṭṭippaṭala muṟpaṭarcci (Glacial Advance) vaṭakōḷattil nikaḻntatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu. ākavē, airōppāvi lēṉum vaṭa amerikkāvilēṉum māntaṉ piṟantakam irun tirukka muṭiyātu.
mutaṟkāla māntaṉukkēṟṟa veppamum iyaṟki vaḷamum, naṇṇilak kōṭṭic (Equator) cārnta veppa nāṭṭilēyē irukka muṭiyum. attakiya nāṭu muḻukippōṉa kumarināṭē.
māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu allatu paṭikkappaṭṭatu eṇṇiṟ keṭṭāta toṉmik kālamātalāl, attakiya toṉmi vāyntatum kumarināṭē.
iṟṟi nila amippiṉpaṭi, iru tiṇiyuyirikaḷum pāreṅkum paraviyirukkum nilimi, kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭiyi māntaṉ piṟantakamākak koṇṭāltāṉ viḷaṅkum.
3. kumarināṭē tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam (tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000)
(1) intiyāviṟkuḷḷēyē vaḻaṅkum tamiḻ uḷḷiṭṭa tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ allatu teṉṉintiya moḻikaḷ, teṟkil varavarat tiruntiyum colvaḷam mikkum tūymippaṭṭum ilakkiya vilakkaṇamuṟṟum ceṟintum uḷḷaṉa.
tirāviṭac coṟkaṭkellām perumpālum mūlamum muntiya vaṭivum tamiḻilēyē uḷḷaṉa.
(2) paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa mutal karu eṉṉum iruvakip poruḷkaḷum, iṉṟum teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kuriyaṉa vākavē uḷḷaṉa.
(3) paṇṭit tamiḻar iṟanta pōṉa tam muṉṉōrit teṉpulattār eṉṟē kuṟittaṉar.
teṉpulattār teyvam viruntokkal tāṉeṉṟāṅ
 kimpulattā ṟōmpal tali		(kuṟaḷ.43)
teṉpula vāḻnark karuṅkaṭa ṉiṟukkum
 poṉpōṟ putalvar peṟāa tīrum		(puṟam.9)
(4) paṇṭit tamiḻkkaḻakaṅkaḷ mūṉṟaṉuḷ, mutaliraṇṭum muḻukippōṉa kumarināṭṭilēyē iruntaṉa.
(5) ki. pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiliruntavarum, ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ iḷavalārum, palkalip perumpulavarum, muṟṟat tuṟanta muḻumuṉivarum, naṭunili tiṟampātavarumāṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ,
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
 kumarik kōṭuṅ koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa
 vaṭaticik kaṅkiyum imayamuṅ koṇṭu
 teṉṟici yāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ vāḻi	(cilap. 11:19-22)
eṉṟum, kaṭikkaḻakak kālap pulavaruḷ oruvarāṉa neṭṭimiyār,
cennīrp pacumpoṉ vayiriyark kītta
 munnīr viḻaviṉ neṭiyōṉ
 naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē		(puṟam.9)
eṉṟum, talikkaḻakat teṉmaturiyit taṉ karimēṟ koṇṭirunta  kumarināṭṭup paḵṟuḷiyāṟṟiyum, ak kālattup pāṇṭiyariyum pāṭiyuḷḷār.
(6) naṭavaracaṉ tilli maṉṟu, tamiḻ ñālattiṉ neñcat tāvākiya naṭuviṭamākavē pāṇṭiyaṉāl amikkappeṟṟatu.
tillimaṉṟu vaṭapāṟ paṉimalikkum teṉpāṟ kumari malikkum naṭuviṭattil amintataṉālēyē, pērulakattiṉ neñcattāvi nikarttatāyiṟṟu.
(7) cilappatikāra vēṉiṟkāti mutalaṭikku varinta vuriyil,
toṭiyōḷ - peṇpāṟ peyarāṟ kumariyeṉpa tāyiṟṟu. ākavē, teṉpāṟ kaṇṇatōr āṟṟiṟkup peyarām. āṉāl, neṭiyōṉ kuṉṟamum toṭiyōḷ natiyumeṉṉātu puvamu meṉṟatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, mutalūḻiyiṟutikkaṇ teṉmaturi yakattut taliccaṅkattu....... nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpat toṉpatiṉmar eṇṇiṟanta paripāṭalum mutu nāriyum mutukurukum kaḷariyāviriyu muḷḷiṭṭavaṟṟip puṉintu terintu nālāyirattu nāṉūṟṟu nāṟpatiṟṟiyāṇṭu irīiyiṉār kāyciṉa vaḻuti mutaṟ kaṭuṅkōṉ īṟāyuḷḷār eṇpattoṉ patiṉmar ......... akkālattu avar nāṭṭut teṉpāli mukattiṟku vaṭavelliyākiya paḵṟuḷi yeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkum kumari yeṉṉu māṟṟiṟkumiṭiyē eḻunūṟṟuk kāvatavāṟum, ivaṟṟiṉ nīrmalivāṉeṉa malinta ēḻteṅka nāṭum, ēḻmaturināṭum, ēḻmuṉpālināṭum, ēḻpiṉ pāli nāṭum, ēḻkuṉṟanāṭum, ēḻkuṇakāri nāṭum, ēḻ kuṟumpaṉi nāṭum eṉṉum inta nāṟpattoṉpatu nāṭum, kumari, kollam mutaliya paṉmali nāṭum kāṭum natiyum patiyum taṭanīrk kumari vaṭaperuṅ kōṭṭiṉkāṟum, kaṭal koṇ ṭoḻitalāṟ kumariyākiya puvameṉṟā reṉṟuṇarka. eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār ciṟappukkuṟippu varintuḷḷār. itu avar kaṭṭik kūṟiya ceytiyāka irukka muṭiyātu.
ivaṟṟāl, kumarināṭē tamiḻ tōṉṟiya iṭam allatu tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam eṉpatu, teritaru tēṟṟamām.
4. kumarikkaṇṭap parappu
oru mutaṉmiyāṉa iṉaviyal uṇmiyum ciṟanta poruṭpāṭuḷḷatum etuveṉiṉ, pōrṉiyōviluḷḷa tayakkar eṉṉum marapiṉar maramēṟuvatippaṟṟi uvālēcu varintuḷḷa varaṇaṉi, teṉṉintiya āṉimali vāṇarāṉa kāṭarukku ovvoru nuṇkuṟippilum muṟṟum poruntu mātalāl am maliyilum eḻutappaṭalām eṉpatē. intiyat tīvakkuṟiyil (Peninsula), eṉakkut terintavari, kāṭariṭiyum tiruvāṅkūr mali vēṭariṭiyum maṭṭum peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkum vaḻakkam, veṭṭuppaṟkaḷ ellāvaṟṟiyum allatu avaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi maṭṭum, arampam pōlaṉṟik kūrnuṉik kuviyamākac cīvikkoḷ vatākum. intac cīval, kāṭariṭiyē piyaṉkaḷukkup patiṉeṭṭām akaviyilum peṇpiḷḷi kaṭkup pattām akaviyil allatu atiyaṭuttum, uḷikoṇṭō aṟuvāḷ koṇṭō araṅkoṇṭō ceyyappaṭuvatākac collap paṭukiṉṟatu. kīṟṟum (Skeat) piḷākutaṉum (Blagden) maliyāt tīvakkuṟic cākkuṉar (Jakuns) tam paṟkaḷik kūrākac cīvikkoḷḷum vaḻakkattik kavaṉittirukkiṉṟaṉar. tiru. kirāpōrṭu  (Crawford) maliyāt tīvakaṇattil palli arāvuvatum kaṟuppākkuvatum tirumaṇattiṟku munti nikaḻa vēṇṭiya caṭaṅkeṉṟum, palli yarāviyiruppatu oru peṇ mūppaṭintiruppatik kāṭṭum potuvāṉa vaki yeṉṟum namakkut terivikkiṉṟār. par. (Dr.) kē. ti. (K.T) pireyacu (Preuss) oru kaṭṭuriyil malākkāviluḷḷa ciṟunīkarōvariṉ mūṅkiṟ cīppukaḷiluḷḷa kōlaṅkaḷi nuṭpamāka varaṇittu, avaṟṟit teṉṉintiyak kāṭar aṇiyum mūṅkiṟ cīppukaḷiluḷḷa viyakkat takkavāṟu ottuḷḷa kōlaṅkaḷuṭaṉ oppu nōkkukiṉṟār. antak kōlam, nāṉ coṉṉavāṟu aṇiyiyal vaṭivaḷavik kōlamaṉ ṟeṉṟum, paṭaveḻuttukaḷiṉ toṭareṉṟum, oru koḷkiyi nuṇṇitāka viḷakkik kāṭṭukiṉṟār. kīṟṟum piḷākutaṉum cemaṅkup (Semang) peṇṭir tam muṭiyil aṇiyum viyappāṉa cīppuvaki, muṟṟilum nōykkāppākavē aṇivatākat tōṉṟukiṉṟa teṉṟu kavaṉittuk kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. intac cīppukaḷ perumpālum mūṅkilālēyē ceyyappaṭṭu, kaṇakkaṟṟa kōlaṅkaḷāl aṇi ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ enta iraṇṭum eṉṟēṉum muṟṟum ottatilli. ovvoru nōykkum oru ciṟappukkōlam uriyatākac collappaṭṭatu. attakiya cīppukaḷ perakkiluḷḷa paṅkaṉ (Pangan) cemaṅku cākki (Saki) marapiṉarālum, cemaṅkucākkik kalappu marapiṉaruḷ māperumpāṉmiyarālum, aṇiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. tiru. viṉceṉṟu (Vincent) avarukkut terintavari, kāṭar cīppukaḷ nōykkāppākak karutappaṭavilli yeṉṟum, avaṟṟiluḷḷa varivukaḷ mantirak kuṟippuk koṇṭaṉa vallaveṉṟum, eṉakkut terivittirukkiṉṟār. oru kāṭaṉ eppōtum oru cīppuc ceytu, taṉ maṉivikkut tirumaṇattiṟku muṉpu allatu tirumaṇac caṭaṅkiṉ iṟutiyil, koṭuttal vēṇṭum. iḷaṅkāḷiyar tammuḷ yār ciṟanta cīppuc ceyya muṭiyumeṉṟu, oruvarō ṭoruvar ikalikkoṇṭu cey kiṉṟaṉar. cila camiyaṅkaḷil avarkaḷ cīppukaḷiṟ putumiyāṉa uruvaṅkaḷip poṟikkiṉṟaṉar. eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tiru. viṉceṉṟu oru cuvarkkaṭikāra mukappi mika naṉṟākap poṟitta oru cīppip pārttiruk kiṉṟār. 5
kumarikkaṇṭam kiḻakkil āttirēliyāviyum mēṟkil teṉṉāppirikkāviyum vaṭakkil intiyāviyum taṉṉuṭaṉ iṇittukkoṇṭirunta perunilap parappāta lālum, māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatiliruntu tamiḻar muḻunākarikam aṭintatuvari, ēṟattāḻa īrilakkam āṇṭu makkaḷ atiṟ kuṭiyiruntataṉālum, an nilavāṇar palvēṟu nākarika nilippaṭṭavarāyum palvēṟu moḻiyiṉarāyum iruntaṉar. āyiṉum, mēṟpākattiṉar perumpālum tamiḻarum kīḻppākattiṉar perumpālum nākarum
5. C. T. S. I. Int. pp. xxi - ii
āvar. nākarikarum anākarikarumāka nākar irucārār. nākarika nākaruḷ oru vakuppārē kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ perumpakuti mūḻkiya piṉ amerikkāviyaṭintu māyā nākarikatti vaḷartta tākat terikiṉṟatu.
5. kumarināṭṭut tamiḻ (tōrā. 1,00,000-50,000)
ulakil mutaṉmutal tōṉṟiya tiruntiya moḻi tamiḻē.
moḻiyamippu
uṇarcciyolikaḷ (Emotional Sounds), viḷiyolikaḷ 
(Vocative Sounds), oppolikaḷ (Imitative Sounds), kuṟippolikaḷ (Symbolic Sounds), vāycceykiyolikaḷ (Gesticulatory Sounds), kuḻavivaḷarppolikaḷ (Nursery Sounds), cuṭṭolikaḷ (Deictic Sounds) eṉṉum eḻuvaki yolikaḷik koṇṭa iyaṟki moḻi (Natural Language) allatu muḻittal moḻi (Inarticulate Speech) muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭintapiṉ, paṇpaṭṭa moḻi (Cultivated Language) allatu iḻittal moḻi (Articulate Speech) tōṉṟiṟṟu. muṟiyē cēymiyaṇmi muṉmiyuṇarttum ā, ī, ū eṉṉum muccuṭṭukaḷiṉiṉṟē, iḻittal moḻi perumpālum uruvāyiṟṟu. am mūṉṟaṉuḷḷum muṉmic cuṭṭākiya ūkāramē, tamiḻc coṟkaḷuḷ mukkāṟ paṅkiṟku mēṟpaṭṭa pakutiyip piṟappittuḷḷatu.
īkārattiṉiṉṟu ēkāramum ūkārattiṉiṉṟu ōkāramum, mōṉit tirivākat tōṉṟiṉa. mutaṟkaṇ neṭilākavē tōṉṟiya uyirkaḷ piṉṉark kuṟilākak kuṟukiṉa. akaramum ikaramuñ cērntu ai eṉṟum, akaramum ukaramuñ cērntu au eṉṟum, iru puṇarolikaḷ (Diphthongs) eḻuntaṉa. avi oliyaḷavil muṟiyē ay, av eṉa olittaṉa.
kocci vaḻakkil, ili eṉpatu eli eṉṟum uṉakku eṉpatu oṉakku eṉṟum olippatiṉiṉṟu, ēkāra ōkāram allatu ekara okaram mōṉit tirivākat tōṉṟiyami aṟiyappaṭum.
itaḻ kuvintolikkum muṉmic cuṭṭāṉa u eṉṉum vitiyeḻuttu, lakarameyyīṟu peṟṟu ul eṉṉum mūla vēriyum ataṉoṭu coṉmutal meykaḷ cērnta kul, cul, tul, nul, pul, mul eṉṉum vaḻivērkaḷiyum tōṟṟuvikka, av veḻu vēriṉiṉṟum mūlavaṭiyum vaḻiyaṭikaḷum tirintu, muṉmi, muṉvarutal (tōṉṟutal), muṉcellal (cellal), neruṅkutal (kūṭutal), poruntutal, vaḷital, tuḷittal, turuval ākiya eṇperuṅ karuttukaḷiyum, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa kiḷik karuttu kaḷiyum, āyirakkaṇakkāṉa nuṇkaruttukaḷiyuṅ koṇṭu, māperum pāl tamiḻc coṟkaḷip piṟappittuḷḷaṉa.
ovvoru nāṭṭilum, ulaka vaḻakkumoḻi potu makkaḷ amippum, ilakkiyam pulamakkaḷ amippum ākum. kumarināṭṭu makkaḷ nākarikam mutirntu paṇpaṭṭavarum nuṇmatiyiṉarumā yiruntataṉāl, ellā eḻuttoli kaḷiyum cevviyāka olittum, ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum viṉikaḷiyum nuṭpamāka vēṟupaṭutti avaṟṟiṟ kēṟpac ciṟappuc coṟkaḷi vaḻaṅkiyum, pakuttaṟivaṭippaṭiyiṟ poruḷkaḷip pākupaṭuttiyum, tam moḻiyi vaḷarttum vaḻaṅkiyum vantaṉar.
oliyeḷimi
avar ulakamutal māntaṉiṉattārātaliṉ, avar vāyiṟ piṟkālattilum piṟamoḻikaḷilum tōṉṟiya vallolikaḷ eḻavēyilli. tamiḻiluḷḷa ka ca ṭa ta pa vum camaṟkirutam eṉṉum vaṭa moḻiyiluḷḷa ka (kka) ca (cca) ṭa (ṭṭa) ta (tta) pa (ppa)vum, vaṉmiyil ottaṉavalla. tamiḻkkakaram iraṭṭittāl tāṉ vaṭa moḻit taṉikkakaratti ottolikkum. āṅkilatti lum aṅṅaṉamē. `maker' eṉṉum col, mēkkar eṉṟē iraṭṭitta kakaravoli koṇṭiruttal kāṇka. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi valliṉamum.
mūccoliyum (Aspirate Sound) taṉitta eṭuppoliyum tamiḻuk kiṉmiyāl, mūccoli koṇṭa valleḻuttō eṭup poli (Voiced Sounds) koṇṭa taṉi valleḻuttō tamiḻil illavēyilli. melliṉa meykaḷiṉ piṉ aṭuttu varum valliṉa meykaḷē, tamiḻil eṭuppoli koḷḷum.
mūccoliyum taṉiyeṭuppoliyuṅ koṇṭa teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam mutaliya tiraviṭamoḻikaḷ, kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻiṉiṉṟē tirintaṉavēṉum, mikamikap piṟkālat taṉa vātaliṉ av vāriyat taṉmiyaṭintaṉaveṉa aṟika.
iv vuṇmi yaṟiyātār, tamiḻilum mutaṟkālattil eṭup polikaḷ (g, j, d|, d, b,) iruntu, piṉṉar eṭuppilāvoli kaḷāka māṟiṉa eṉap pitaṟṟuvar. tamiḻ tōṉṟiyatu kumari nāṭeṉṉum uṇmiyaṟiyiṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟār. eṭuppilā voli eṭup poliyāka māṟumēyaṉṟi, eṭuppoli eṭuppilāvoliyākat tiriyātu. piñcu muṟṟik kāyāvatē yaṉṟi, kāy iḷantu mīṇṭum piñcā vatilli. māntaṉ varalāṟṟil tamiḻaṉ ciṟupiḷḷi pōṉṟavaṉātaliṉ, avaṉ vāyiṟ piṟkāla māntariṉ valloli kaḷ eḻavilli.
tamiḻiluḷḷa cakaram, camaṟkirutak kicukicuppolikaḷuḷ 
(Sibilants) mutalatiyē ottatākum. ati eṭuppum mūccoliyu muḷḷa (Voiceless Unaspirate) camaṟkiruta aṇṇa valleḻut tiṟkuc (Palatal Surd) camamākak koḷvatu. tamiḻiyalpi aṟiyāmi yēyām.
ḻa, ṟa potumakkaḷē tōṟṟuvitta olikaḷātalāl, avi taṉittum colluṟuppākavum varumpōtellām, avaṟṟi avarkaḷ muḻuc cevviyākavē olittu vantaṉar.
coṟpuṇarcciyum potumakkaḷ ceyalē. teṟkut teru, kaṟṟāḻi, mutumakkaṭṭāḻi, pāṟcōṟu, āṭṭi vāriyam, aṟṟik kūli, karumakkuṟi eṉa mūvakit tiripu puṇarcciyiyum, pēccilum eḻuttilum iṟukak kaṭip piṭittaṉar.
poruṭpākupāṭu
ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum uyir, mey, uyirmey (uyirōṭu kūṭiya mey) eṉa mūṉṟākap pakuttum; avaṟṟuḷ uyiri nilittiṇi iyaṅkutiṇi eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttum; avaṟṟuḷ nilittiṇiyip pul, pūṇṭu, ceṭi, koṭi, maram eṉap palavākap pākupaṭuttiyum; avaṟṟiṉ uṟuppukaḷi vēr, tūr, aṭi, kavi, (kompu, kiḷi, pōttu, kuccu) ili, pū, kāy eṉap pirittum; avaṟṟuḷ iliyit tāḷ, tōki, ōli, tōṭu, ili, aṭi eṉṟum; pūvi arumpu, pōtu, malar, vī, cemmal eṉṟum,  kāyip piñcu, pirukku, kāy, kaṉi, neṟṟu, vaṟṟal eṉṟum; nuṇṇiya vakiyil vēṟupaṭuttiyum; piñciyum kaccal (vāḻi), vaṭu (mā), mūcu (palā) kurumpi (teṉṉi, paṉi) eṉat taṉittaṉi ciṟappup peyariṭṭum vaḻaṅkiṉar.
poruḷkaḷip pōṉṟē viṉikaḷiyum nuṇṇitāka vēṟupaṭutti, ciṟappuc coṟkaḷāl, avaṟṟik kuṟittaṉar.
e-ṭu :
viḻittal 	= 	kaṇ tiṟattal, kaṇ tiṟantu maruḷutal
pārttal	=	maṉak kuṟippiṉṟi iyalpākap pārttal, 			pārttatip pārttal
kaṇṇuṟutal	= 	kuṟippoṭu pārttal
nōkkutal	= 	kūrntu allatu kavaṉittup pārttal
nōṅkutal	= 	vīṭṭuttici nōkki viruppoṭu 				pārttal
nōṭutal	= 	āyntu pārttal
kāṇutal	= 	tēṭiṉati allatu virumpiyavarip 				pārttal
taṉmi muṉṉilip peyarkaḷellām taṉvēṟṟumip paṭṭē (Orthoclite) akkālattu vaḻaṅkiṉa.

ulaka vaḻakkil, iḻintōṉ, ottōṉ, uyarntōṉ eṉṉum  muttiṟattik kuṟikka nī, nīm, nīṅkaḷ eṉṉum muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ ēṟpaṭṭuviṭṭataṉāl, avaṟṟuḷ nīṅkaḷ eṉpati yoṭṭit taṉmiyilum ēṅkaḷ eṉṉum iraṭṭip paṉmic col tōṉṟiviṭṭatu.
ēṉ, ēm eṉpaṉa iṟṟit tamiḻil eḻuvāyāka vaḻaṅkā viṭiṉum, viṉimuṟṟīṟukaḷāka vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
e-ṭu:	vantēṉ, vantēm.
illiruntu, iṉiṉṟu eṉṉum 5ām vēṟṟumiyuru pukaḷ, ikkālattiṟpōl iṭattiliruntu iṭattiṉiṉṟu eṉṟum vaḻaṅkiyirukkalām.
`ē' eṉṉum aṭi `yā' eṉṟu tirintapōtu, yāṉ, yām, yāṅkaḷ eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. āyiṉ, avi vēṟṟumi yēṟkātu eḻuvāyaḷavil niṉṟuviṭṭaṉa, ātalāl, avaṟṟiṟkum ēm, ēṅkaḷ eṉpavaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumi vaṭivukaḷē uriyavāyiṉa.
`yā' eṉṉum tirivaṭi `nā' eṉṟu tirintapōtu, nāṉ, nām, nāṅkaḷ eṉṉum vaṭivukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. avi piṉ varumāṟu vēṟṟumiyēṟṟaṉa.

	ēṅkaḷ eṉpataṟkuc coṉṉa kuṟippi nāṅkaḷ  
	eṉpataṟkuṅ koḷka.
nāṅkaḷ eṉpatu, iṟṟit tamiḻil muṉṉiliyi uḷappaṭuttātu tavaṟāka yāṅkaḷ eṉpataṟkup pakaramāka (patilāka) vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.

7.	niṉṉiṭam	nimmiṭam	niṅkaḷiṭam
nīṉ eṉṉum orumippeyar, piṉṉar nī eṉa īṟukeṭṭu, ulaka vaḻakkiṟ peruvaḻakkākavum ilakkiya vaḻakkil muḻu vaḻakkākavum vaḻaṅkat talippaṭṭatu. nīṉ eṉṉum peyar vaḻakki, iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭṭu nāṭārkula vaḻakkiṟ kāṇalām. kaṉṉaṭattil ap peyar ukaravīṟu peṟṟu nīṉu eṉa vaḻaṅku kiṉṟatu.
nīm eṉṉum paṉmippeyarum, iruvaki vaḻakkilum arukiya vaḻakkāyiṟṟu. ataṉāl nī eṉṉum īṟukeṭṭa peyar ir eṉṉum paṭarkkip palarpālīṟu peṟṟu nīyir eṉṟāki nīm eṉpataṟkut talimāṟāka (patilāka) vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
nīm eṉṉum peyar vaḻakki, iṉṟum pāṇṭi nāṭṭuc ciṟṟūrp pakutikaḷiṟ kāṇalām.
nīyir eṉpatu, nāḷaṭivil nīvir eṉat tirintu piṉṉar nīr eṉat tokkatu.
nī, nīr (nīyir, nīvir) eṉṉum iru vaṭivukaṭkum taṉ vēṟṟumiyilli. ataṉāl, muṉṉataṟku ūṉ eṉṉum peyariṉ vēṟṟumiyum,  piṉṉataṟku  nūm  allatu  ūm  eṉṉum peyariṉ vēṟṟumiyum,  vaḻaṅki  varukiṉṟaṉa.  nīr  eṉṉum  vaṭiviṟkuc  coṉṉatē,  nīyir,  nīvir eṉṉum vaṭivukaṭkum okkum.
teṟkil ēṟattāḻa īrāyiraṅ kal tolivu parantirunta kumarināṭu muḻukippōṉamiyāl, atil vaḻaṅkiya āyirak kaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. mutaliru kaḻakattālum iyaṟṟappaṭṭum pōṟṟappaṭṭum vanta āyirak kaṇakkāṉa taṉittamiḻ nūlkaḷum aḻikkappaṭṭuviṭṭami yāl, avaṟṟil vaḻaṅkiya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. ātalāl, mēṟkāṭṭiya taṉmi muṉṉilip peyarkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṟkum, avaṟṟiṉ vēṟṟumi vaṭivukaṭkum, iṉṟu  eṭuttukkāṭṭilliyeṉa aṟika.
eṭṭiṟkumēl aṭutta eṇṇiṉ peyar akkālattil toṇṭu eṉṟē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.

palukkaṟ cemmi
kaṇ, māṉ, ūr, kal, kūḻ, āḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, eṇ, īṉ, pār, col, umiḻ, kēḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉikaḷum, ukara vīṟṟit tuṇik koḷḷā malum, tāy, vāy eṉpaṉa  pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, cey, cāy eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉikaḷum, ikaravīṟṟit tuṇik koḷḷāmalum olittaṉa.
maram, avaṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, vantāṉ, pōṉāṉ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉikaḷum, iṟuti melliṉa mey mūkkoliyaḷavilaṉṟi muṟṟum cevviyāka olittu vaḻaṅkiṉa.
avaḷ, makaḷ eṉpaṉapōṉṟa peyarkaḷum, vantāḷ, pōṉāḷ eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa viṉikaḷum, iṟuti ḷakaramey olittē vaḻaṅkiṉa.
ilakkaṇa naṭi
irukkiṟatu, iruntatu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paṭarkki yoṉṟaṉpāl viṉikaḷellām, koccivaṭivilaṉṟi ilakkaṇa vaṭivilēyē  vaḻaṅkiṉa.
ellā viṉimuṟṟukaḷum eḻuvāyk kēṟṟa īṟu peṟṟē vaḻaṅkiṉa.

irutiṇip pakuppu
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar paṇpāṭṭilum pakuttaṟivāṟ ṟalilum taliciṟantiruntataṉāl, pakuttaṟi vaṭippaṭiyi lēyē uyarvakuppu tāḻvakuppu eṉa ellāp poruḷ kaḷiyum iruvakuppāka vakuttu, pakuttaṟivuḷḷa uyir meykaḷik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭkē pālīṟu koṭuttum, aḵtillāta uyirmeykaḷik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaṭku eṇṇīṟē koṭuttum, coṟṟoṭar amittaṉar.
e-ṭu: 	uḻavaṉ uḻukiṉṟāṉ.    
	uḻatti kaḷiyeṭukkiṉṟāḷ.
	avaṉ vantāṉ.	uyarvakuppu
	avaḷ pōṉāḷ.
	kāḷi uḻukiṉṟatu.
	āvu mēykiṉṟatu.
	āṇpaṉi vaḷarkiṉṟatu.          		
	peṇpaṉi kāykkiṉṟatu.	tāḻvakuppu
	atu vantatu.
	avi pōyiṉa.
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇam iyampal eṉṉum muṟiyil, ilakkaṇa nūlār pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaḷi uyar tiṇi yeṉṟum, avarallāta uyiruḷḷaṉavum illaṉavu māṉa ellāvaṟṟiyum  aḵṟiṇi yeṉṟum, kuṟittaṉar.
makkaḷip paṭittavarum ellām aṟintavarumāṉa, iṟivaṉ uyartiṇi yeṉpatic colla vēṇṭuvatilli.
iv virutiṇip pakuppu tamiḻukkē ciṟappām. teluṅ kil ittakiya toṉṟiruppiṉum, atu tamiḻip piṉpaṟṟiyatē.
colvaḷam
kumarināṭṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa ulaka vaḻakkuc coṟ kaḷum, ilakkiya vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷum, iṟantupaṭṭa piṉpum, tamiḻ oppuyarvaṟṟa colvaḷam mikka moḻiyāka uḷḷatu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, iṉṟum, collutalik kuṟikka aṟi, iyampu, ici, uri, eṉ, ōtu, kiḷa, kiḷattu, kūṟu, cāṟṟu, ceppu, col, navil, nutal, nuval, noṭi, pakar, paṟi, paṉṉu, paṉuvu, pukal, pēcu, māṟu, miḻaṟṟu, moḻi, viḷ, viḷattu, viḷampu mutaliya coṟkaḷum,
ōṭutalik kuṟikka, ōṭu, kiṇṇu, toṅku, pari mutaliya coṟkaḷum,
yāṉiyik kuṟikka, āmpal, umpal, uvā, eṟumpi, ōṅkal, kaṭamā, kari, kaṟiyaṭi, kuñcaram, kimmali, kimmā, tumpi, tūṅkal, tōl, nālvāy, pūṭki, poṅkaṭi, maṟamali, mātiram, moy, vaḻuvi, vāraṇam, vēḻam mutaliya potuc coṟkaḷum; cinturam, piṇimukam, pukarmukam mutaliya ciṟappuc coṟkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
aṟi mutal viḷampu īṟākak kuṟikkappaṭṭa coṟka ḷellām, collutal eṉṉum potupporuḷil ottaṉa vēṉum, collutal vakiyāṉa ciṟappup poruḷil vēṟu paṭṭaṉavām. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉiyavum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē tiraviṭamoḻikaṭ kellān tāyātalāl, muṉṉataṉ oruporuṭ coṟkaḷiṉiṉṟē, piṉṉavaṟṟuḷ ov voṉṟum ovvoru collit terinteṭuttāḷukiṉṟatu. itu tāy vīṭṭiluḷḷa, oru viṉikkup payaṉpaṭum palvakik kalaṅkaḷuḷ ovvoṉṟi ovvoru makaḷum eṭuttup puḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟatu.
e-ṭu: 	ceppu 	- 	ceppu (teluṅku)
       	kiḷa 	- 	hēḷu (kaṉṉaṭam)
     	paṟi 	- 	paṟa (maliyāḷam)
ceppu eṉṉum viṉiccol, tamiḻil viṭi collutal eṉṉum ciṟappup poruḷilum, teluṅkiṟ collutal eṉṉum potup poruḷilum vaḻaṅkum. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉic coṟkaḷum taṉtaṉ ciṟappup poruḷi iḻakkum.
paṟital eṉṉum col, ētampaṟin talla ceytu (tiv. tiruvāy. 4: 6: 8) eṉṟu ilakkiya vaḻakkilum, collāmaṟ paṟiyāmal ōṭip pōyviṭṭāṉ eṉṟu ulaka vaḻakkilum vaḻaṅkutal kāṇka.
vīṭṭik kuṟikkuñ coṟkaḷuḷ, il eṉṉum teluṅkuc collum maṉi yeṉṉum kaṉṉaṭac collum maṭṭumaṉṟi, camaṟkirutattilum piṉṉiya (Finnish) moḻi kaḷilum vaḻaṅkum kuṭiyeṉṉum collum tamiḻiluḷḷa teṉṟu, tamiḻiṉ oruporuṭ colvaḷattik kālṭu velār viyantiruttali nōkkuka.
camaṟkirutam pirākiruta moḻikaḷiṉiṉṟum tamiḻiṉiṉ ṟum, āṅkilam ulaka moḻikaḷuṭ palavaṟṟiṉiṉṟum, ērāḷa mākak kaṭaṉ koṇṭiruppatāl, avaṟṟiluḷḷa oruporuṭ colvaḷatti viyattaṟkilli.
tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷ iṟṟit tamiḻil illiyeṉiṉ, avi kumari nāṭṭut tamiḻil vaḻaṅki iṟantupaṭṭaṉa eṉṟaṟital vēṇṭum. colliṟkuc coṉṉatē colvaṭiviṟkum.
colvaṭivukaḷuḷ muṉṉatip piṉṉatiṉiṉṟu vēṟu paṭuttiyaṟitaṟku,  moḻiyārāycciyoṭu kūṭiya collā rāycci vēṇṭum.
	nāl-nālku - nāṉku
te.	nāluku (g), ka. nālku (k)
vāluḷip puravi nālkuṭaṉ pūṭṭi		(perumpāṇ. 489)
nālku eṉpatu tamiḻvaṭivē.
tūymi
mutaliru kaḻakak kālattilum āriyar teṉṉāṭu varāmi yāl, ak kaḻakaṅkaḷ irunta kumarināṭṭut tamiḻ, oliyiṟ pōṉṟē collilum poruḷilum ilakkiyattilum karuttilum muḻut tūymi peṟṟiruntatu. ilakkiyattuḷ ilakkaṇamum aṭaṅkum.
6. kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar (tōrā. ki.mu. 50,000-10,000)
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar, perumpālum aintiṇippaṭṭa nāṭṭup puṟattārum, ciṟupāṉmi tiṇimayakkuṟṟa nakara vāṇarumāka, iruvaki vāḻcciyarāy iruntuvantaṉar.
kuṟiñcinili māntar, kolliyum paṟampum pōṉṟa malikaḷiyaṭuttuk kūṭṭaṅkūṭṭamākak kuṭicikaḷil taṅki, vēṭṭiyāṭiyum kiḻaṅkakaḻntum tēṉeṭuttum tiṉi viḷittum, muraṭṭuk kampaḷiyuṭuttum murukaṉi vaṇaṅki yum, vāḻntu vantaṉar. uṇavup poruḷkaḷic cuṭuvataṟkum eḷiya  muṟiyiṟ camippataṟkum, iraviṟ koṭiya vilaṅku kaḷi ōṭṭuvataṟkum, tīkkaṭi kōlāl tīyuṇṭākkik koṇṭaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppuk kuṟicciyeṉṟum ciṟu kuṭiyeṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kuṟiñcinila makaḷir kuṟicolvatu kuṟam eṉappaṭṭatu. ataṉāṟ kuṟavar eṉṟum, vēṭṭiyāṭuvatāl vēṭṭuvar eṉṟum, kuṟiñcinila māntarkku irupeyarkaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa.
mullinila māntar, pulveḷiyuḷḷa kāṭukaḷi yaṇṭi, āṭumāṭerumiyākiya munnirikaḷiyum vaḷarttu  pāl, tayir, mōr, veṇṇey, ney ākiya āṉinti marutanilattum pāli nilattum viṟṟum, varaku, cāmi, avari tuvari mutaliya vāṉāvārip payirkaḷi viḷittum, pañcāṭiyum meṉ kampaḷiyum uṭuttum, pulvaḷarccikku iṉṟiyamiyāta maḻipoḻiyum mukil vaṇṇaṅ koṇṭa māyōṉ (kariyōṉ) eṉṉum teyvatti vaṇaṅkiyum, vāḻntu vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu, pāṭiyeṉṟum cēriyeṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
āyar, iṭiyar, potuvar, toḻuvar, toṟuvar, kōvar, kōvalar eṉpaṉa mullinilattār peyarkaḷ.
kō (āṉ) mēyppatil vallavar kōvalar āṉ vallōr eṉṟu tivākarattilum, āṉvallavar eṉṟu cūṭāmaṇiyilum,  kuṟikkappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka.
kaṇṇaki kaṇavaṉākiya kōvalaṉ peyarē kōpāla eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripākum. atilum kō eṉpatu teṉcollē.
kāṭṭumāṭukaḷip piṭippataṟkum vīṭṭumāṭukaḷi aṭakku taṟkum  maṟavali vēṇṭiyiruntataṉāl, āyar tam makaḷirik kollēṟu piṭittaṭakkiya iḷiñarkkē maṇañceytu koṭuttaṉar. kollēṟaṭakkal ēṟutaḻuval eṉappaṭṭatu. āṭṭiṭiyar (pulliṉat tāyar) eṉṟum māṭṭiṭiyar (nalliṉattāyar) eṉṟum, āyar iru vakiyarāyp pirintaṉar.
marutanila makkaḷ nilavaḷamum nīrvaḷamum mikka iṭaṅkaḷil taṅki, nel karumpu vāḻi mutaliya naṉceyp payirkaḷiyum cōḷam kēḻvaraku mutaliya puṉceyp payirka ḷiyum viḷittu, pañcāṭiyum paṭṭāṭiyum kampaḷiyā ṭiyum aṇintu, vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) eṉṉum vāṉavar kōṉi vaṇaṅki, maṇṇāluṅ  kallāluñ ceṅkalālum kaṭṭiya maccuvīṭukaḷilum ōṭṭu mukaṭṭu vīṭukaḷilum kūri vīṭukaḷilum vatintu, niliyāka vāḻntu  vantaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu maṭṭum ūr eṉṟu peyarpeṟṟatu.
ur-uṟu. uṟutal = poruntutal, nilattōṭu poruntu tal, niliyākak kuṭiyiruttal. ur=uṟu. ur-ūr. o.nō; 
uṇ-ūṇ.
payirttoḻilil mutalviṉi uḻavātalāl, at toḻilum uḻaveṉappaṭṭatu. atic ceytavar uḻavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar; pōraṭikkuṅ kaḷattil vēli ceyvatu paṟṟik kaḷamar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉar.
perunilakkiḻārellārum piṟarik koṇṭē viṉi ceytu, vīṭṭiliruntu niḻalil vāḻntataṉāl, caṟṟu veḷuttup pōy veḷḷāḷareṉṟum veṇkaḷama reṉṟum; ciṟunilak kiḻārum  nilamilikaḷum kaṭuveyililuṅ kāṭṭilum vēli ceytu karuttup pōṉatiṉāl, kārāḷareṉṟum karuṅkaḷamareṉṟum collap paṭṭaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, uḻutuṇ pār, uḻuvittuṇpār eṉa uḻavar iru vakuppārāyiṉar.
marutanila makkaḷ niliyāka vāḻntataṉāl, avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppukaḷ nāḷaṭiviṟ pērūrum mūtūrumāyiṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila nakarum nakaramum āyiṉa. nakaram mānakar.
uḻavarukkup pakkattuṇiyāka neyavar, kuyavar, taccar,   kollar, paṟampar (tōlviṉiñar), mayirviṉiñar, vaṇṇār, cekkār mutaliya patiṉeṇ toḻilāḷarum, piṟa nuṇviṉiñarum; paṭippaṭiyākat tōṉṟiṉar. nakarattil nākarikan tōṉṟi vaḷarntatu.
kaṭalnīrāl uppu viḷittaṟkum, āṟu kuḷaṅkaḷ nīr vaṟṟiyapiṉ kaṭalmīṉ piṭittaṟkum, muttuk kuḷittaṟkum, cila kūṭṭattār neytalnilattil vatintaṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyirup puk kuppam, kari, tuṟi, kāyal, kāṉal eṉap palvēṟu peyar peṟṟaṉa.
neytalnilattār, iṭattiṟkum karuvikkum toḻiluk kum ēṟpa, kariyār eṉṟum, paṭavar - paravar eṉṟum, mukkuvar eṉṟum, aḷavar eṉṟum, peyarpeṟṟaṉar. avar vaṇaṅkiyatu vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum kaṭal teyvam.
mulliyuṅ kuṟiñciyum kaṭuṅkōṭik kālattiṟ cila viṭaṅkaḷil vaṟṟivaṟaṇṭu viḷiccaliṉṟip pañcanili ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, pāliyeṉappaṭṭaṉa. attakiya camiyaṅ kaḷil, an nilavāṇar vaḻippaṟikkavum akkam pakkaṅkaḷiṟ koḷḷiyaṭikkavum nērntatu. mūvēntarum avarit tam paṭi maṟavarākkiyum, tām pakitta aracariṉ nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu ānirikaḷik kavarntuvara ēviyum, vantaṉar.
pālinila vāṇar kaḷḷar, maṟavar, eyiṉar, vēṭṭuva eṉap palavakuppiṉar. avarkaḷ kuṭiyiruppu kuṭikkāṭu, nattam, paṟantali, cēri eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avarkaḷ teyvam kāḷi.
marutanilap pērūrkaḷil, mutaṟkaṇ ovvoru toḻi lāḷarum tattam viḷiporuḷkaḷiyum ceyporuḷ kaḷiyum nelliṟkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytu vantaṉar. piṉṉar ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum vāṅkiviṟkum vaṇikar tōṉṟiṉar. mutalil uḷḷūrp poruḷ viṟpaṉi yum, piṉṉar veḷiyūrp poruḷ viṟpaṉiyum, ataṉpiṉ veḷināṭṭup poruḷ viṟpaṉiyumāka, vaṇikam vaḷarntu vantatu. nilavāṇikattiṉ piṉ nīrvāṇikam tōṉṟiṟṟu. nila vāṇikar cāttuvar eṉṟum, nīrvāṇikar nāvikar (nāykar) eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉar.
makkaḷtoki perukiyapiṉ kaḷavuṅ kavarvum mikunta taṉāl poruṭkāppiṟkum vaḻakkut tīrppiṟkum āṭcikkum kāvalaṉ ēṟpaṭṭāṉ. kuṭumpattalivaṉ nilimiyil toṭaṅkiya āṭcip patavi, ūrttalivaṉ, vēḷ (kuṟunila maṉṉaṉ), kō (perunila aracaṉ), vēntaṉ, māvēntaṉ (pēraracaṉ) eṉap paṭippaṭiyāka vuyarntatu. vēntaṉ ēṟpaṭṭa piṉ avaṉukkaṭaṅkiya ciṟṟaracarkku makuṭam aṇiyum urimi illātu pōyiṟṟu. iyalpākavē paṇṭamāṟṟiṉāl ēṟpaṭṭa tiṇimayakkam, aintiṇi  nilaṅkaḷiyum cērt tāṇṭa vēntaṉ kālattil, mika virivaṭintatu. vēyntāṉ - vēyntaṉ - vēntaṉ = muṭiyaṇin tavaṉ. vēytal = talimēlaṇital. iṅṅaṉam kuṟunila aracar, peru nila aracar eṉa āḷvār iruvakiyarāyiṉar. pāṇṭiyaṉ cōḻaṉ cēraṉ eṉṟa muṟiyil mukkuṭi vēntar  tōṉṟi, āṭcikku marutanilattil oṉṟum, nīrvāṇikattiṟku neytal nilattil oṉṟumāka, iru mānakaramittu āṇṭaṉar. marutanilat talinakarellām pērāṟṟaṅkari mēlēyē amintiruntaṉa.
nākarikam vaḷarntu pātukāppum ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, uḻuvit tuṇṇum uḻavarāṉa veḷḷāḷar tam oḻivu nēraṅkaḷiṟ kalviyi vaḷarttu vantaṉar. immikkuriya ulakiyaṟ kalvikkup piṉ, maṟumikkuriya  mataviyaṟ kalvi tōṉṟiṟṟu. ulaka vāḻkkiyil veṟuppuṟṟavar tuṟavi mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. kaṟṟōruḷ illaṟattār nūlkaḷip pārppataṉāṟ pārppār allatu pārppaṉar eṉṟum, tuṟavaṟattār ellāvuyir kaḷiṭattum aruḷ pūṇṭamiyāl antaṇar eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉar. iṅṅaṉam, kaṟṟōr pārppārum antaṇarum eṉa irutiṟattārāyiṉar.
cilar, pārppaṉar eṉṉum col pirāmaṇaṉ eṉṉum vaṭa colliṉ tiripeṉṟum, tuṟavu nili pirāmaṇaṉukkēyuriya teṉṟum,  pitaṟṟip pētiyari  ēmāṟṟuvar.  kumari  nāṭṭut tamiḻar kālam āriyar eṉṉum iṉak karuvē tōṉṟāta kālam. pāṭṭaṉip peṟṟa pūṭṭaṉ maṇavāta iḷamik kālattil koṭpēraṉ eṅṅaṉam tōṉṟa villiyō, aṅṅaṉamē kumarināṭṭut tamiḻaṉ kālattilum āriyaṉ tōṉṟavilli. ataṉāl avaṉ tirimoḻiyum tōṉṟavilli.
kaṇṇuṭiyar eṉpavar kaṟṟōr mukattiraṇṭu
 puṇṇuṭiyar kallā tavar	(kuṟaḷ. 33)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiṉiṉṟu, pārppāṉ eṉṉum peyark karaṇiyat tiyum,
antaṇar eṉpōr aṟavōrmaṟ ṟevvuyirkkum
 centaṇmi pūṇṭoḻuka lāṉ	(kuṟaḷ. 30)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiṉiṉṟu, antaṇaṉ eṉṉum peyark karaṇiyat tiyuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka.
cilar, pārppaṉaṉ eṉṉum colvaṭivu pirāmaṇaṉ eṉpatip piṉpaṟṟiya teṉpar. āṉ, aṉaṉ eṉa 
iru vakiyīṟukaḷip peṟuvatu tamiḻ viṉimuṟṟukaḷiṉ iyalpē.
e-ṭu:
i.kā. 	ni.kā. 	e.kā.
pārttaṉaṉ 	pārkkiṉṟaṉaṉ 	pārppaṉaṉ
pārttaṉaḷ 	pārkkiṉṟaṉaḷ 	pārppaṉaḷ
pārttaṉar 	pārkkiṉṟaṉar 	pārppaṉar
pārttaṉṟu 	pārkkiṉṟaṉṟu 	--------
pārttaṉa 	pārkkiṉṟaṉa 	pārppaṉa
aṉatu - aṉṟu (aṉ + tu). pārttaṉṟu = pārttatu.
marutanilattil uḻavar, vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār eṉṉum nāl vakuppārum tōṉṟiyapiṉ. vēḷāṇmi ceytu  viruntōmpuvatil veḷḷāḷarum kārāḷarumāṉa iruvaki yuḻavarum taliciṟantataṉāl, vēḷāḷar eṉap paṭṭaṉar.
tiṇimayakkam
marutanilattuk kōnakarkaḷum talinakarkaḷum nakaraṅ kaḷāka (mānakarkaḷāka) virivaṭintapōtu, mulli nilamum cilaviṭattuk kuṟiñcinilamum paṭippaṭiyāka aṭuttu vantaṉa. kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉam pōṉṟa neytal nakaraṅkaḷil, maruta mum mulliyuṅ kalantaṉa. cēra nāṭṭu vañci pōṉṟa nakaraṅ kaḷil aintiṇiyuṅ kalantaṉa.
neytal nakaraṅkaḷellām paṭṭiṉam eṉṟum, avaṟṟiṉ pirivukaḷ pākkam eṉṟum peyarpeṟṟaṉa.
marutanilat tūrkaṭku, mutaṟkaṇ, neytalnila māntar uppum mīṉum caṅkum pavaḷamum pōṉṟa kaṭalpaṭu poruḷ kaḷiyum,   mullinila māntar āṉintiyum, kuṟiñcinila māntar tēṉum iṟicciyum maruppum (tantamum) kācaṟiyum (kastūriyum) pōṉṟa malipaṭu poruḷkaḷiyum, koṇarntu nelliṟkum āṭikkum māṟṟiṉar. piṉṉar, piḻippiṟkākac cilar nakar toṟum vantu kuṭiyēṟiṉar. pāli nila maṟavarum kāvaltoḻiṟ kamarntaṉar.
tirunāḷ, perunāḷ, porunāḷ ākiya munnāḷ      kaḷilum, aintiṇi makkaḷum kalantaṉar. aṅṅaṉaṅ kalantu uriyāṭum pōtu, marutavāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ ūr etuveṉṟum, mullivāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ pāṭi (allatu cēri) etuveṉṟum, kuṟiñcivāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ kuṟicci etuveṉṟum, neytal vāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ kuppam (allatu tuṟi) etuveṉṟum, pālivāṇaṉi nōkki uṉ kuṭikkāṭu etuveṉṟum, viṉavuvatē iyalpum vaḻakkamum. pērūrkaḷilum, mūtūrkaḷilum pala toḻilāḷarum nilattārum kalantu, tiṇimayakkam mikutiyāka ēṟpaṭṭapōtu, aintiṇik kuṭiyiruppum ūr eṉṉum potuppeyar peṟṟaṉa.
kulavakuppu
mutaṟkaṇ, nilantoṟum oru vakuppāka aintiṇik kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
piṉṉar marutanilattil, uḻavarkkup pakkat tuṇiyākap patiṉeṇ kittoḻiṟ kulaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
ataṉpiṉ vaṇikar, aracar, pārppār eṉṉum kulaṅ kaḷ allatu kuṭikaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. aracar eṉṟatu, vēntarum vēḷirum pōla vaḻivaḻiyāka vanta aracar kuṭumpaṅkaḷiyē.
kalvic ciṟappum oḻukka vuyarvum paṟṟip pārppārum cilaviṭattu antaṇar eṉap peṟṟaṉar.
ovvoru kulamum nāḷaṭivil iraṇṭum palavumākak kiḷittup palkiṟṟu.
ellāk kulaṅkaḷum toḻilaṭippaṭiyilēyē tōṉṟiyiyaṅkiṉa. oruvaṉ ettoḻiliyum mēṟkoḷḷa lām. ceyyun toḻilpaṟṟiyē oruvaṉ kulam  amintatu. āyiṉ, tantiyaṟivum āṟṟalum maṉappāṉmiyum iyal pākavē makaṉukku amintu viṭṭataṉālum, ovvoru vaṉum piḷḷip paruvattilēyē peṟṟōṉip piṉpaṟṟi oru toḻiliṟ payiṟci peṟṟuviṭṭataṉālum, talimuṟi toṟum maraput toḻiṟṟiṟami vaḷarcciyaṭintu vantata ṉālum, makkaḷtoki perukap peruka ellāt toḻilāḷarkkum piḻippu ēṟpaṭṭataṉālum, perum pālum peṟṟōr toḻiliyē makkaḷ vaḻivaḻi ceytu varuvārāyiṉar.
ūrkkiḻavaṉ (ūraṉ), ūrāḷi, ūrkkuṭumpaṉ, ūrk kāmiṇṭaṉ (kavuṇṭaṉ), nāṭṭāṇmikkāraṉ, nāṭaṉ, nāṭāṉ, nāṭṭāṉ, periyataṉakkāraṉ, ampalakāraṉ, cērvikāraṉ, mūppaṉ, maṉṟāṭi, paṇṇiyāṭi, mutali, talivaṉ, tēvaṉ eṉa iṭattiṟkum kulattiṟkum ēṟpap pala peyar peṟṟirunta ūrttalivaṉ patavi pōṉṟē, aracar patavikaḷum tolvaravākat toṭarntu vantaṉa.
iyalpāṉa uṟavaṉpum poruḷutaviyum pātukāppum nōkki, potumakkaḷ perumpālum uṟaviṉarkkuḷḷēyē koḷvaṉiyuṅ koṭuppaṉiyuñ ceytu vantaṉar. aracarkkō evvaki vilakkum taṭiyum iruntatilli. vēḷir makaḷiriyum maṟakkuṭi makaḷiriyum ayalnāṭṭaracar makaḷiriyum avar maṇantu vantaṉar.
ekkulattārāyiṉum, tuppuravum oḻukkamumē uṇṭāṭṭuṟa viṟkuk kavaṉikkappaṭṭaṉa. kalvi, oḻukkam, tavam, atikāram,  celvam, īki, maṟam, āṟṟal eṉpavaṟṟā laṉṟip piṟappāṟ ciṟappilli.
atti, matti, ātaṉ, pūtaṉ, utiyaṉ, titiyaṉ, kaṇṇaṉ, paṇṇaṉ, kāṭaṉ, kōṭaṉ, cāttaṉ, kūttaṉ, nākaṉ, pēkaṉ, naṉṉaṉ,  poṉṉaṉ, nāṇaṉ, vāṇaṉ, vēntaṉ, cēntaṉ, naḷḷi, kiḷḷi, pāri, kāri, piṭṭaṉ, vaṭṭaṉ, tattaṉ, tittaṉ, mūlaṉ, vēlaṉ, iṟiyaṉ, poṟiyaṉ, mūvaṉ, tēvaṉ, koṟṟaṉ, veṟṟaṉ, marutaṉ, virutaṉ, cēraṉ, kīraṉ, vaṅkaṉ, koṅkaṉ, veḷḷiyaṉ, piḷḷiyaṉ, karuppaṉ, poruppaṉ, pacci, cecci, maliyaṉ, kaliyaṉ, māṟaṉ, vīṟaṉ, nallaṉ, cellaṉ, nampi, tampi, appaṉ, kuppaṉ, vēṭṭaṉ, cēṭṭaṉ mutaliyaṉavākat taṉittum puṇarntum aṭiyaṭuttum varum peyarkaḷ ettoḻilā ruṭiyaṉa vāyiṉum, ikkālattu vaḻaṅkum kulappaṭṭamiṉṟi, ēṉi nāṭṭār peyarkaḷ pōṉṟē akkālattu vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
vaṇṇār, aṭuttōr (mayirviṉiñar) mutaliya ūrkkuṭi makkaḷ, paṟampar (tōlviṉiñar) pōṉṟē, orē vakuppārāy ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntaṉar.
mēṉāṭukaḷiṟ pōṉṟē, uṇavuvakiyiṉāl ēṟṟat tāḻ villātiruntatu.
ellāt tirukkōvilkaḷilum tamiḻ oṉṟē vaḻipāṭṭu moḻiyāyiruntatu. uvaccar, kurukkaḷ, tirukkaḷ, pulavar, paṇṭāriyar, nampimār, pōṟṟimār, cāttuvār mutaliya  tamiḻp pūcāriyarē, civaṉ tirumāl kāḷi eṉṉum mut teyvak kōvilkaḷilum vaḻipāṭu naṭatti vantaṉar, kōviṟ pukavurimiyum vaḻipāṭṭurimiyum ellā vakuppārkkuñ camamāyiruntatu. kōviliyaṭuttirunta tirukkuḷattiṟ kuḷittapiṉ allatu kikāl kaḻuviyapiṉ, ellārum kāṇikkiyoṭu kōviṟkuṭ pukuntaṉar.
tiruviḻākkaḷilum puṉalāṭṭu viḻākkaḷilum ellā vakup pārum  kalantē koṇṭāṭiṉar. āṟukuḷaṅkaḷil vakuppiṟ kōriṭamāka variyaṟukkappaṭavilli.
iṭukāṭu ellā vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntatu. piṇattic cuṭuvatu tamiḻar vaḻakkamaṉṟu.
vēḷāḷar, vaṇikar, aracar, antaṇar eṉpatē iyaṟki muṟiyāyiṉum, poruḷilakkaṇa nūlār, ākkavaḻippāṟṟal paṟṟiyum mukkāla aṟivupaṟṟiyum muḻuttūymipaṟṟiyum antaṇari muṉṉum, iṟivaṉpōl ellāriyum muṟi ceytu kāttalpaṟṟi aracari iraṇṭāvatum, nāṭṭi vaḷam paṭuttiyum pōrkkālattil aracarkkup poruḷ koṭuttum utavum vaṇikari mūṉṟāvatum vittu, antaṇar aracar vaṇikar vēḷāḷar eṉat talikīḻākac ceyaṟki muṟip paṭuttiṉar.
7. kumarināṭṭu nākarikam
nākarikam eṉpatu tiruntiya paḻakkavaḻakkam. atu mutaṉ mutal nakarattil allatu nakariyil tōṉṟiṉataṉāl nākarikam eṉappaṭṭatu.
o.nō:
	L. civis = citizen, civil = polite, civil-civilize-civilization =             				advanced stage in social development.
	L. urbis = city, urbane = courteous, urbanity = polished manners.
akanākarikam, puṟanākarikam eṉa nākarikam iruvakip paṭum. akanākarikamāvatu tiruntiya oḻukkam; puṟanākarika māvatu tiruntiya ulaka vāḻkki. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu paṇpāṭu eṉṟum, piṉṉatu aṭiyiṉṟip potuvāka nākarikam eṉṟum collappaṭum.
nākarikam
uṟiyuḷ
vēntarum kōkkaḷum māperuñcelvarum vatiyum nakaraṅkaḷum, vēḷirum maṇṭilat talivarum vatiyum nakar kaḷum, kallālum ceṅkalālum cuṇṇāmpuk kāriyiṭṭuk kaṭṭic cippi nīṟṟuc cutiyāl tīṟṟiya, māṭa māḷikikaḷum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum koṇṭu, māṭciyāṉa kāṭciyaḷittaṉa.
cippic cutiyiṉ veṇmic ciṟappi,
``oḷiyārmuṉ oḷḷiya rātal veḷiyārmuṉ
 vāṉcuti vaṇṇaṅ koḷal''	(kuṟaḷ. 714)
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷālum,
``putuppiṟi yaṉṉa cuticey māṭattu''	(puṟam. 378)
eṉṉum puṟanāṉūṟṟu aṭiyālum,
``varuṅkuṉṟa moṉṟurit tōṉṟilli yampala vaṉmalayat
 tiruṅkuṉṟa vāṇa riḷaṅkoṭi yēyiṭa reytalemmūrp
 paruṅkuṉṟa māḷiki nuṇkaḷa pattoḷi pāyanummūrk
 karuṅkuṉṟam veṇṇiṟak kañcukam ēykkum kaṉaṅkuḻiyē.''
eṉṉun tirukkōvic ceyyuḷālum (15), aṟiyalām.
māṭaṅkaḷilellām kaṇkavar vaṇṇa vōviyaṅkaḷ tīṭṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
``cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunili maṉitoṟum
 miyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
 evvaki yuyirkaḷum uvamaṅ kāṭṭi
 veṇcuti viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya
 kaṇkavar ōviyam''	(3: 127-131)
eṉṟu maṇimēkaliyum,
``māṭakkuc cittiramum''
eṉṟu naṉṉūṟpāyiramum kūṟutal kāṇka.
nakarantoṟum vāṉaḷāvum eḻunilik kōpuramum kaṭṭappaṭṭiruntatu. mutaṟkaṇ aracarvatiyum araṇmaṉik kāvaṟ kōpuramāka irunta kaṭṭaṭam, piṟkālattil vāṉaḷāvun tirukkōviṟ kōpuramāka vaḷarntatu.
kō = aracaṉ, puram = uyarnta kaṭṭaṭam.
``puriyuyar vākum.'' 		(tol, uri. 4)
(kōnakarun talinakarum kōṭṭi matilāluñ cūḻap paṭṭiruntaṉa. kōṭṭiyuḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ puri eṉṟum, kōpura muḷḷa nakaraṅkaḷ puram eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.)
purital = vaḷital. puri = vaḷinta (cūḻnta) kōṭṭi yuḷḷatu. puri - purici = kōṭṭi matil.
matil, eyil, iñci, cō eṉa matilaraṇ nālvakip paṭṭiruntatu. mika uyaramāṉatu matil; muṟṟukiyiṭum pakivar mītu ampeyyum ēvaṟikaḷiyuṭiyatu eyil; cempi yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiyiṟukiyatu iñci; ariya kaṭum poṟikaḷiyuṭiyatu cō.
kōnakarkaḷil aṅkaṇa nīrik kaṇṇiṟ paṭāmaṟ pōkkuvataṟku, karantupaṭiyeṉṉum puticālakam irun tatu. atu maṟuku (perunteru) naṭuviṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu yāṉik kūṭṭam mēṟcellumpaṭi, karuṅkallāl mūṭappaṭṭiruntatu. atiṟ ceṉṟanīr yāṉit tutikki pōṉṟa tūmpiṉ vāyilāy akaḻiyil viḻuntatu.
aracar kuṭumpattuṭaṉ pōrkkālattil tappikkoḷ vataṟku, araṇmaṉiyiṉ kīḻum kōnakarkaḷiṭiyum, curaṅkap pātikaḷ amikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
āṭi
pañcu, paṭṭu, mayir ākiya mūvakik karuvip poruḷālum iḻikkappaṭṭa nuṇṇūliṉāl, pūvitaḻ pōṉṟa meṉmiyum,  iḻi pirittaṟiyāc ceṟivum, pāmpuc caṭṭiyum mūṅkiṟ coliyum nīrāviyum naṟumpukiyum otta naṉitava noymmiyum, kaṇkavar palvaṇṇap pūttoḻilum, uṭiya palvaki āṭikaḷ aṟuvakip paruvattiṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu neyyappaṭṭaṉa.
mayir eṉṟatu, āṭṭumayiriyum oruvaki eli mayiriyum.
ūṇ
ulakil mutaṉmutal uṇavi nākarikamāyc camittuṇ ṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
uṇṇal tiṉṉal nakkal parukal eṉṉum nālvaki ūṇ viṉiyum oruṅkē cērntatu cāppiṭutal.
uṇavic cōṟum kuḻampum kaṟiyum eṉa mūṉṟākap pakuttu, nellariciyic cōṟākac camittu, kuḻampiyum kaṟiyiyum kaṇṇiṟku aḻakum mūkkiṟku naṟumaṇamum nāviṟku iṉcuviyum uṇarttuvaṉavum, uṭampi valup paṭuttu vaṉavum, nōyvarātu taṭuttu vāḻnāḷi nīṭṭip paṉavumāṉa palavaki maruntuc carakkukaḷic cērttu ākki, mutaliṟ kuḻampum iṭiyil miḷakunīrum iṟutiyil tayir allatu mōrum ūṟṟi, iyaṟkiyāṉa melliya vāḻiyiliyil aṟucuvi yuṇṭi toṉṟutoṭṭu iṉpamākac cāppiṭṭu varupavaṉ tamiḻaṉē.
arici vakiyil, nellarici tali; varaku tiṉi cāmi kutiri vāli kāṭikkaṇṇi ākiyavaṟṟiṉ arici iṭi; kañci kaḷi kūḻukku maṭṭum utavum cōḷam kampu kēḻvaraku kaṭi.
nellariciyil cīrakac campā ciṟumaṇic campā eṉpavi tali; piṟa campā iṭi; maṭṭiyarici kaṭi.
cōḷam kampu kēḻvarakuṇṇum pāṭṭāḷi makkaḷum cuviyāṉa kuḻampum toṭukaṟiyum virumpuvar.
kuḻampiṟ kutavum payaṟṟu vakikaḷuḷ, tuvari tali;  avari mocci pacci iṭi: uḻuntu taṭṭāṉ kal karampi koḷ (kāṇam) kaṭi.
(miḷaku) nīr, nīṭṭāṇam (soup), cāṟu, kuḻampu, kūṭṭu eṉpaṉa kuḻampu vakiyil muṟiyē oṉṟiṉoṉṟu tiṇṇiyavi.
palvakik kāykaḷum mīṉum iṟicciyum, vāṭṭal, vatakkal, vaṟuval, poriyal, tuvaṭṭal, puraṭṭal, aviyal, oṭiyal eṉap   palvēṟu muṟiyil kaṟiyākkappaṭṭaṉa.
kuḻampiṟkum kaṟikkum iṟutiviṉi tāḷippu (ucilippu). ataṟku eṇṇeyum āneyyum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
iṟicci vakiyil, muḻuvuṭumpu, mukkāl kāṭi, ari kōḻi, kāl āṭu eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
aṇi
poṉṉum muttum maṇiyum kumarināṭṭil ērāḷa māyk kiṭittaṉa. nuṇṇiya ōviya vēlippāṭuḷḷa aṇikaḷum kalaṅkaḷum taṭṭumuṭṭukaḷum uruvaṅkaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.
kittoḻil
ellāt toḻilkaḷiyuñ cevviyākac ceytaṟku, irumpiṉālum cempiṉālum veṇkalattiṉālum ciṟanta karuvikaḷ ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. irumpu mutaṉmutal tamiḻakat tilēyē kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku, ataṉ tamiḻp peyar āriya moḻikaḷil vaḻaṅkuvatē cāṉṟām.
	E. iron, OE. iren, isen, Ger. eisen, Skt. ayas.
makkaṭ karuttukaḷ
ovvoruvarum pāl vakuppu vēṟupāṭiṉṟi aṟam poruḷiṉpam vīṭu aṭital vēṇṭum. entavūrum contavūr. ellārum ōriṉam. aṟivāṟṟal kuṇañceyal kaḷālaṉṟip piṟappāṟ ciṟappilli. oruvaṉukkup perumi yum ciṟumiyum taṉṉālēyē varum. iṉpamum tuṉpamum varutaṟku avaravar paḻaviṉiyē kāraṇam; piṟarallar. piṟantaviyellām iṟakkum. ataṟku añcal kūṭātu. atu oru nilimiyiṉiṉṟu  iṉṉoru nilimikkup puku vāyilē. ovvoruvarkkum vāḻkkiyil oru kaṭamiyuṇṭu. atu niṟivēṟiyapiṉ iṟappu varum.
uṭaluḻippiṟkum maṉavuḻippiṟkum uyarpatavikkum tāḻpatavikkum ēṟṟavāṟu, kuṇaṅkaḷum tiṟamikaḷum piṟappilēyē amintuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ātalāl, periyōrip pukaḻtalum ciṟiyōri ikaḻtalum takkataṉṟu.
ovvoruvarum tammip pōṟ piṟarik karututal vēṇṭum. iṟantapiṉ uṭaṉ varuvaṉa avaravar ceyta iruvaki viṉikaḷē.
araciyal
aracar tammi uyirākavum tam kuṭikaḷi uṭampākavum karuti, aimperuṅkuḻu, eṇpērāyam, aivaki uṟutic cuṟṟam, nālvakip paṭi, nālvaki yaraṇ ākiyavaṟṟit tuṇik koṇṭu, naṭuniliyāka muṟiceytu ceṅkōlāṭci ceytu vantaṉar.
potukkalvi
ikkālattiṟpōl araciyaluṟuppāṉa potukkalvit tiṇikkaḷam akkālattil illiyēṉum, ēṟattāḻa ellārum tamiḻil eḻutap paṭikkat terintavarāyiruntaṉar. pāṭṭāḷiyuṭpaṭap pala toḻilārum pāvalarākavu miruntaṉar.
ceyyuḷ
akkālat tilakkiya mellām ceyyuḷākavē yiruntatu. atu veṇpā, āciriyappā (akavaṟpā), kalippā, vañcippā eṉṉum nālvakip pākkaḷāka vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. ceyyuḷ nūliṟkup poruḷ kūṟum uriyum eḷiya ceyyuḷākavē yiruntatu. veṇpāviṟkum kalippāviṟkum iṇiyāṉa yāppuvaki  vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇaviyalātu.
māttiri, eḻuttu, aci, cīr, aṭi, yāppu, marapu, tūkku, toṭi, nōkku, pā, aḷavu, tiṇi, kikōḷ, kūṟṟu, kēṭpōr, kaḷaṉ, kālam, payaṉ, meyppāṭu, eccam, muṉṉam, poruḷ, tuṟi, māṭṭu, vaṇṇam eṉac ceyyuḷuṟuppu kaḷ irupattāṟākak koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
aṟiviyal
ilakkaṇam
ettuṇic ciṟantaṉavēṉum, ēṉi yuyartaṉic cemmoḻikaḷilellām eḻuttu, col, yāppu, aṇi eṉa moḻiyilakkaṇam nālvakippaṭṭatē. tamiḻilakkaṇam maṭṭum, ulakil mutaṉmutal iyaṟṟappaṭṭatēṉum, coṟ ṟoṭarum ceyyuḷum nūlum kūṟum poruḷukkum ilakkaṇaṅ kaṇṭatākum.
akam, puṟam eṉap poruḷ irupāṟpaṭum. avaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum evvēḻu tiṇikaḷik koṇṭatu. potuvāka, akam kātal vāḻkkiyiyum, puṟam pōr viṉiyiyum kūṟum. ivi yiraṇṭumallāta ellāp poruḷkaḷum, vāki yeṉṉum puṟattiṇikkuḷ aṭakkap paṭum.
kaṇakku
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar kaṇakkil mikat tērcci peṟṟiruntaṉar.
oṉṟiliruntu mēṟpaṭṭatu mēlvāyilakkam eṉṟum,  oṉṟiṟkuk kīḻppaṭṭatu kīḻvāyilakkam eṉṟum collap paṭṭaṉa.
kumpam = nūṟu kōṭi
caṅkam (caṅku) = ilakkaṅ kōṭi
tāmari = kōṭā kōṭi
vāraṇam = nūṟu kōṭā kōṭi (kōṭā kōṭi?)
ivaṟṟiṉ matippu vēṟuvakiyākavuñ collappaṭum. kuvaḷi, veḷḷam, āmpal, neytal eṉpaṉavum aṭukkiya kōṭikaḷik kuṟikkum pēreṇkaḷākum. kīḻvāyilakkam mukkāl, ari, kāl, arikkāl, mākāṇi (vīcam), mā 
(1/20), kāṇi (1/80), muntiri (1/320), kīḻmuntiri (muntiriyil 
1/320) eṉpaṉa.
ciṟṟilakkattiṟkum pērilakkattiṟkum perukkal vāy pāṭiruntatu pōṉṟē, catura vāypāṭum iruntatu. atu kuḻikkaṇakku eṉappaṭṭatu. ciṟṟilakkak kuḻippuc ciṟukuḻi yeṉṟum, pērilakkak kuḻippup peruṅkuḻi yeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kaṇiyam
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻak kaṇiyar nuḻi matiyaruṅ kūrṅ kaṇṇarumāyiruntataṉāl puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟali, mūtiri, kaḻi, koṭiṟu, aravu, koṭunukam, kaṇi, uttaram, ki, aṟuvi, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉi, tuḷaṅ koḷi, kuruku, muṟkuḷam, kaṭikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākki, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉṉum irupatteḻu nāḷkaḷiyum; mēḻam, viṭi, āṭavi, alavaṉ, maṭaṅkal, kaṉṉi, tuli, naḷi, cili, cuṟavam, kumpam, mīṉam eṉṉum paṉṉīrōrikaḷiyum; ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ, cevvāy, aṟivaṉ (putaṉ), viyāḻaṉ, veḷḷi, kāri (caṉi) eṉṉum ēḻu kōḷ kaḷiyum, kaṇṭu, kaṇiya nūli vaḷarttu vantaṉar.
eḻukōḷkaḷiṉ peyarāl eḻunāṭkiḻami (vāram) vakukkappaṭṭatu. atu ulaka muḻutum paravi iṉṟuvari vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
kiḻamip peyarkaḷ camaṟkirutattilum, ōrip peyarkaḷ camaṟkirutattilum ilattīṉilum, moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭuḷa.
iṉṉici
mural (curam), paṇ, meṭṭu, tāḷam eṉṉum nāṟkūṟu koṇṭa iṉṉici muḻuvaḷarcci yaṭintiruntatu.
paṇ (eḻumural), paṇṇiyal (aṟumural), tiṟam (aimmural), tiṟattiṟam (nāṉmural) eṉa nālvakippaṭṭa paṇkaḷ narappaṭivāl 11,991 ākak kaṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. āyappāli, vaṭṭap pāli, caturappāli, mukkōṇap pāli (tirikōṇap pāli) eṉṉum nālvaki muṟiyil, eḻuperumpālikaḷum avaṟṟiṉ kiḷikaḷum tirikkap paṭṭaṉa. at tirivu muṟikaḷ, muṟiyē muḻumural, ari mural, kāl mural, arikkāl mural ākiya muralnilikaḷit taḻuviyaṉa eṉpar. in nuṭpaṅkaḷ iṟṟi icivāṇarkkut teriyāvāṟu, āriyattāl maṟiyuṇṭu pōyiṉa.
tōl tuḷi narampu uṟi (kañcam) eṉṉum nālvaki icikkaruvikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu narappuk karuvi. narappuk karuvikaḷuḷ ciṟantatu yāḻ eṉṉum vīṇi. yāḻkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻ. ataṉ vaḻiyiṉatē iṟṟi vīṇi. viṇṇeṉal = narampu teṟittal. viṇ-vīṇ-vīṇi. tōliṟ ciṟantatu mattaḷam (periyatu) allatu mataṅkam (mirutaṅkam). tuḷiyiṟ ciṟantatu pullāṅkuḻal.
nāṭakam
iciyoṭu naṭamum naṭippum cērntatu nāṭakam. 
naṭi + akam = nāṭakam.
naṭam eṉpatu, kati taḻuviyatum taḻuvātatum eṉa iruvakittu. nāṭakam eṉpatu potuvāka ulakavaḻakkiṟ kati taḻuvi varum naṭippiyē kuṟikkum. kūttu eṉṉum peyar naṭattiṟkum nāṭakattiṟkum potu. naṭam (naṭaṉam) eṉpatu āṭal eṉṟum peyarpeṟum.
āṭalkaḷ, nilittaṉavum avvappōtu āṭalā ciriyaṉāṟ putituputitāyp payiṟṟappaṭuvaṉavum eṉa irupāṟ paṭum. nāṭakamellām putituputitāyt tōṉṟuvaṉavē.
āṭalkaḷ vēttiyal, potuviyal, tēviyal eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. kaṭiyam, marakkāl, kuṭi, tuṭi, alliyam, mal, kuṭam, pēṭu, pāvi, pāṇṭaraṅkam, koṭṭi eṉṉum patiṉōrāṭalum, piṟkālattil teyvaṅkaḷ āṭiṉavākak katikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa. kuravi, vari eṉpaṉavum tēviyaliṉ pāṟpaṭum.
naṭakkaraṇaṅkaḷ aṟupattu nāṉku. naṭaviṉik ki, oṟṟikki (piṇṭikki) iṇikki eṉa iruvakippaṭum. oṟṟikki muppattumūṉṟu; iṇikki patiṉintu.
naṭippiṟkuriya naḷinayam (apinaya) muppattiraṇṭu.
ceyyuṭkum iṉṉicikkum nāṭakattiṟkum uriya cuvikaḷ: naki, aḻuki, iḷivaral, maruṭki, accam, perumitam, vekuḷi, uvaki eṉa eṭṭu.
iṉṉiciyilum nāṭakattilum kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar, āḻntu īṭupaṭṭum ciṟanta tērcci peṟṟum nirampa iṉpuṟṟum vantataṉāl, iṉṉici nāṭakattiyum tamiḻoṭu cērttu, iyaṟṟamiḻ, icittamiḻ, nāṭakattamiḻ eṉa mukkūṟṟatākki muttamiḻ eṉṟaṉar. ittakip puṇarppu vēṟen nāṭṭilu milli.
maruttuvam
ūti pittuk kōḻi yeṉṉum munnāṭiyiyalpi, teyvattaṉmiyāṉa nuṇmāṇ nuḻipulattā laṟintu, ellā nōykaṭkum maruttuvam aṟuvi eṉṉum iruvakip paṇṭuvam ceyvatu tamiḻa maruttuvam. ataṉ taliciṟanta      maruntu irumpu cempu mutaliya poṉṉaṅkaḷit tūya poṉṉākkuvatum, kaḻineṭuṅkālam (1,00,000 āṇṭu ?) vāḻac ceyvatumāṉa muppu (mūvuppu) eṉṉum oruvakik kalaviyuppu. atu ceyyum vaki maṟiyuṇṭu pōyiṟṟu.
mataviyal
kuṟiñcinilattiṟkuriya cēyōṉ (murukaṉ) vaṇakkat tiṉiṉṟu civaṉiyamum, mullinilattiṟkuriya māyōṉ (kariyavaṉ, māl) vaṇakkattiṉiṉṟu māliyamum, ākiya peruntēva matam iraṇṭum vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa.
ōkappayiṟciyālum uḷḷattūymiyālum eṇṇat tiṇmiyālum iṟippaṟṟālum nuṇmi, parumi, noymmi, kaṉati, karutiyatu peṟutal, karutiyaviṭañ cellutal, karutiya vaṭiveṭuttal, kūṭuviṭṭuk kūṭupāytal ākiya eṇvakip peṟṟikaḷip peṟṟa peṟṟiyar (cittar), iru perun tēva matattiṟkum potuvāṉa kaṭavuḷ matattik kaṇṭaṉar. kaṭavuḷ matamāvatu, kālam iṭam vaṭivu kuṇam mutaliya ellāvaṟṟiyuṅ kaṭantu iyalpāṉa muṟṟaṟivum muḻuvallamiyumuḷḷa oru taṉip paramporuḷi, uḷḷattil toḻutal.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar iṅṅaṉam ulakiyalilum mataviya lilum uyarniliyaṭintiruntataṟkuk karaṇiyam, avar neṟṟiyiṟ peṟṟirunta oruvaki aṟivukkaṇ eṉṟum, atu kaṇṇāṟ kāṇātatik kāṇavum kātāṟ kēḷātatik kēṭkavum vallatu eṉṟum, atip piṉṉōr neṭuṅkālam payaṉpaṭuttāmiyāl ati iḻantuviṭṭaṉar eṉṟum, ārāycciyāḷar cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. ik karutti. civa perumāṉukku neṟṟikkaṇ uṇṭeṉṉuṅ kūṟṟu ōraḷavu valiyuṟuttukiṉṟatu.
paṇpāṭu
vēḷāḷar paṇpāṭu
putitāka illattiṟku vantavarkkum vaḻic cellum ayalārkkum ciṟantavuṇavaḷippatum, irappōrkku illi yeṉṉātu īvatum, vēḷāḷar paṇpāṭu.
vaṇikar paṇpāṭu
poruḷkaḷi vāṅkumpōtu aḷavik kūṭṭip peṟāmalum, viṟkumpōtu kuṟittuk koṭāmalum, nērmi yāka vaṇikañ    ceyvatum; uṟuppilikaṭku ūṭṭup puriyum, ayalār taṅkac cattirañ cāvaṭiyum, kaṭṭi vippatum; vaḻip pōkkarkkuc cōliyoṭu kūṭiya kuḷam veṭṭi vippatum; nīrniliyillāk kāṭṭu vaḻikaḷil nelli maraṅkaḷ vaḷarttu vippatum vaṇikar paṇpāṭu.
aracar paṇpāṭu
kāṭcik keḷiyarāyum kaṭuñcolla rallarāyumirup patum, naṭuniliyāka muṟiceytu ceṅkōlāṭci celuttu vatum, vaḻakkiḻantavar tammiṭam muṟiyiṭa araṇmaṉi vāyilil ārāyccimaṇi kaṭṭivippatum, vaṟaṭcik kālattil vari nīkkuvatum, tirunāḷ peru nāḷkaḷil ciṟiñari viṭutali ceyvatum, matampaṟṟiya uṇmiyi makkaḷaṟitaṟkup paṭṭimaṉṟam naṭattuvatum, kūṉar kuṟaḷar mutaliya eccap piṟavikaḷi araṇmaṉik kuṟṟēvaṟ kamarttuvatum, pōr toṭaṅkumuṉ peṇṭir piḷḷikaḷ piṇiyāḷar mutiyōr mutaliyōrip pātukāppāṉa iṭattiṟku akaṟṟiviṭuvatum paṭikkalamiḻantavaṉum eḷiya paṭikkalamuḷḷavaṉum kīḻē viḻuntavaṉum muṭikulinta vaṉum āṭiyaviḻntavaṉum tōṟṟōṭukiṉṟavaṉum ākiyōr mītu paṭikkalam ēvāmiyum, tōṟṟa aracaṉit tiṟikaṭṭac ceytu avaṉoṭu maṇavuṟavu koḷvatum, pōril mutukiṟ puṇpaṭṭa pōtum vāḻkkiyil māṉaṅ keṭa nērntapōtum uṇṇānōṉpiruntu uyirtuṟap patum, aracar paṇpāṭām.
pulavar paṇpāṭu
aracar matiyātu tarum paricilip peṟātu pōvatum, vaṟumiyiṟ cemmiyāy oḻukutalum, pērāciyiṉmiyum, aracar tīṅkuceyyiṉ taṭuppatum, avarkku añcātu aṟivuri kūṟuvatum potunalam pēṇuvatum, coṟ poḻivaraṅkiṟkuc celliṉ iṟutivariyiliruntu kēṭpatum pulavar paṇpāṭām.
8. kumarināṭṭu ilakkiyam
ilakkiyam (1) eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyam, (2) eḻutappaṭā ilakkiyam eṉa iruvakippaṭum. eḻutappaṭṭa ilakkiyamum,                    (1) urinaṭiyilakkiyam (2) ceyyuḷilakkiyam eṉṟum,                           (1) potuvilakkiyam (2) ciṟappilakkiyam eṉṟum, naṭi paṟṟiyum payaṉpaṭuttum makkaḷpaṟṟiyum ivviru vakippaṭum.
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻilakkiyam, naṭipaṟṟic ceyyuḷilak kiyamum payaṉpaṭuttuvārpaṟṟip potucciṟappilakkiyamu mākum.
potuvilakkiyam
pāṭṭu, uri, nūl, vāymoḻi allatu mantiram, pici, aṅkatam, mutucol eṉṉum eḻuvaki nilikkaḷattiṟ ceyyuḷilakkiyam tōṉṟiṟṟu. nilikkaḷam yāppu vaki,
avaṟṟuḷ : pāṭṭu eṇvaki vaṉappākap pakukkap paṭṭatu.
eṇvaki vaṉappukaḷ
1. ammi
ciṉmeṉ moḻiyāṟ cīrpuṉin tiyāppiṉ
 ammi tāṉē aṭinimir piṉṟē.	(tol.cey.233)
2. aḻaku
ceyyuḷ moḻiyāṟ cīrpuṉin tiyāppiṉ
 avvaki tāṉē aḻakeṉap paṭumē.  	(tol.cey.234)
3. toṉmi
 toṉmi tāṉē
  uriyoṭu puṇarnta paḻimi mēṟṟē.      (tol.cey.235)
4. tōl
 iḻumeṉ moḻiyāṉ viḻumiyatu nuvaliṉum
  paranta moḻiyāṉ aṭinimirn toḻukiṉum
  tōleṉa moḻi toṉmoḻip pulavar.        (tol.cey.236)
5. viruntu
 viruntē tāṉum
  putuvatu kiḷanta yāppiṉ mēṟṟē. 	(tol.cey.237)
6. iyipu
 ñakāra mutalā ṉakāra vīṟṟup 
  puḷḷi yiṟuti iyipeṉap paṭumē.	(tol.cey.238)
7. pulaṉ
``cēri moḻiyāṟ cevvitiṟ kiḷantu
 tērtal vēṇṭātu kuṟittatu tōṉṟiṉ
 pulaṉeṉa moḻipa pulaṉuṇarn tōrē.''	(tol.cey.239)
8. iḻipu
``oṟṟoṭu puṇarnta valleḻut taṭakkātu
 kuṟaḷaṭi mutalā aintaṭi yoppit
 tōṅkiya moḻiyāṉ āṅṅaṉam oḻukiṉ
 iḻipiṉ ilakkaṇam iyinta tākum.''	(tol.cey.240)
ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟukūṭa ikkālattilli. cilappati kāramum maṇimēkaliyum ṉakaramayyīṟṟup pāṭṭukaḷik koṇṭiruppatāl, iyipu eṉṉum vaṉappiṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum. āyiṉ, avi tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭaviyalla. tol kāppiyakkālam ki.mu. 6ām allatu 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu; cilappatikāra maṇimēkali kālamō 
ki. pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa āriyamallāta ilakkaṇa vilakkiyaṅkaḷum moḻic ceytikaḷum, mutaliru kaḻakak kālattiṟ kuriyaṉavēyaṉṟit tolkāppiyar kālattiṟkuriyaṉa valla.
 muntunūl kaṇṭu muṟippaṭa eṇṇip
  pulantokut tōṉē pōkkaṟu paṉuval
eṉṟu paṉampāraṉārum, eṉpa, eṉmaṉār pulavar, nuṇṇitiṉ uṇarntōr kaṇṭavāṟē, eṉamoḻipa, otta teṉpa vuṇaru mōrē, ``cevvi teṉpa ciṟantici ṉōrē eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum, cārpunūlāciriyar muṟiyil tolkāppiyaṉārum, kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
iyipu
vaṉappup pāṭṭukaḷ ñ, ṇ, n, m, ṉ eṉṉum aim melliṉa meykaḷuḷ oṉṟil iṟum. cilappatikāra maṇimēkaliyākiya iraṭṭi vaṉappup pāṭṭukaḷ `ṉ` eṉṉum orē meyyil iṟukiṉṟaṉa. oru meykku oru vaṉappu eṉṟu vittuk koḷḷiṉum, aimmeykkum aivaṉappāvatu iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. oṉṟukūṭa iṉṟilli. iṅṅaṉamē ēṉi vaṉappukaḷum.
viruntu
eṉpatu, avvappōtu putituputitākat tōṉṟum palvakip paṉuvalkaṭkum potuppeyar. tolkāppiyat til viruntup paṉuvaleṉṟu oṉṟum vitantu kuṟikkap peṟavilli.
 kuḻavi maruṅkiṉuṅ kiḻava tākum	(tol. puṟat. 29)
eṉpatu piḷḷittamiḻ eṉṉum paṉuvaliyum,
 ūroṭu tōṟṟamum uritteṉa moḻipa(tol. puṟat. 30)
eṉpatu ulā allatu ūriṉṉiciyiyum kuṟikkalām.
uri eṉpatu, viḷaṅkāta nūṉ mūlattiṟkup poruḷ kūṟuvatu. atuvuñ ceyyuḷākavē iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu; āyiṉ, alliyaracāṇimāli, `pavaḷak koṭimāli' eṉpaṉa pōla eḷiya naṭiyil amintiruntatu. urinaṭiyil irukka vēṇṭiya uriyum ceyyuḷil iyaṉṟami, ak kālattut tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ ceyyuḷ tiṟattic ciṟappak kāṭṭum.
nūl eṉpatu aṟiviyal. ilakkaṇam ōr aṟiviyal. ovvoru kaliyilum teriviyal (Theory) paṟṟiyatellām ilakkaṇam pōṉṟa aṟiviyalē.
vāymoḻi allatu mantiram eṉpatu tirumūlar tirumantiram pōlvatu.
mantiram eṉpatu tūya teṉcollē. muṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal. muṉ-maṉ. maṉ + tiram = mantiram. o.nō: maṉ - maṉṟu - mantu - manti = kūṭṭam. iṉṉatu niṟivēṟum allatu uṇmi eṉṟu eṇṇum, eṇṇattiṇmiyoṭu allatu maṉa valimiyoṭu kūṭiya niṟimoḻiyē mantiram.
pici yeṉpatu viṭukati.
aṅkatam eṉpatu kaṇṭaṉanūl allatu maṟuppunūl. atu etirnūl eṉavumpaṭum.
mutucol eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
pāṭṭup pōṉṟē, ēṉi yaṟuvaki yāppuc ceyyuḷum iṉṟilli. paṇṇatti yeṉṉum oruvaki yāppum iṉṟilli. eḻuvaki yāppum veṇpā, akavaṟpā (āciriyappā), kalippā, vañcippā, eṉṉum tūya pākkaḷum, maruṭpā, paripāṭal eṉṉum kalavip pākkaḷum ākiya aṟuvakip pākkaḷāl iyaṉṟaṉa.
pākkaṭkuriya vaṇṇaṅkaḷ : pāa vaṇṇam, tāa vaṇṇam, vallici vaṇṇam, mellici vaṇṇam, iyipu vaṇṇam, aḷapeṭi vaṇṇam, neṭuñcīr vaṇṇam, kuṟuñcīr vaṇṇam, cittira vaṇṇam, nalipu vaṇṇam, akappāṭṭu vaṇṇam, puṟappāṭṭu vaṇṇam, oḻuku vaṇṇam, orūu vaṇṇam, eṇṇu vaṇṇam, akippu vaṇṇam, tūṅkal vaṇṇam, ēntal vaṇṇam, uruṭṭu vaṇṇam, muṭuku vaṇṇam eṉa irupatu.
vaṇṇam, cittiram eṉpaṉa teṉcoṟkaḷē.
vaḷḷutal = vaḷittal, vaḷitteḻututal. vaḷ-vaṇ-vaṇṇam = eḻutum eḻuttu allatu variyum paṭam, eḻuttiṉ niṟam, niṟam pōṉṟa ceyyuḷoli vaki, vaki.
vaḷ-var-vari-varaṇam = eḻuttu, niṟam, vaki, vakuppu, icippā vaki.
o.nō : tiḷ-tiṇ-tiṇṇi. tiḷ-tir-tiraḷ-tiraḷi-tiraṇi.
cettal = ottal. ce + tiram = cettiram - cittiram = ōviyam.
o.nō : centiram - cinturam = civappu, centūḷ, cennīṟu, cempoṟimuka yāṉi.
tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muntiya aṟuvakip pākkaṭkum, irupatu vaṇṇaṅkaṭkum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa akapporuḷ puṟapporuḷ tuṟikaṭkum, iṉṟu eṭuttuk kāṭṭilli. ellām aḻik kappaṭṭu viṭṭaṉa.
ciṟappilakkiyam
iciyum nāṭakamum
tolkāppiyattiṟku muntiyatu akattiyam. atu muttamiḻilakkaṇam.
 ilakkiya miṉṟi ilakkaṇa miṉṟē
  eḷḷiṉ ṟākil eṇṇeyu miṉṟē
  eḷḷiṉiṉ ṟeṇṇe yeṭuppatu pōla
  ilakki yattiṉiṉ ṟeṭupaṭum ilakkaṇam.	(akat.)
ilakkiyaṅ kaṇṭataṟ kilakkaṇa miyampaliṉ 	(naṉ. 141)
eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār.
akattiyam muttamiḻilakkaṇam eṉpatāl, iyaṟṟami ḻilakkiyam pōṉṟē icittamiḻ nāṭakat tamiḻilakkiyaṅ kaḷum av vilakkaṇa nūṟku muṉpiruntiruttal vēṇṭu meṉpatu peṟappaṭum. akattiyam tolkāppiyattiṟkuc cilapala nūṟṟāṇṭu muntiyatēṉum, iṭik kaḻakattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa teṉpati aṟital vēṇṭum. 
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟici nīṭalum
 uḷaveṉa moḻipa iciyoṭu civaṇiya
 narampiṉ maṟiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.	(tol. eḻuttu 33)
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puḷpaṟi
 ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokii
 avvaki piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa.	(tol. akat. 18)
eṉpavaṟṟāl iṉṉiciyilakkiyamum,
nāṭaka vaḻakkiṉum ulakiyal vaḻakkiṉum
 pāṭal cāṉṟa pulaṉeṟi vaḻakkam 	(tol. akat. 53)
eṉpataṉāl nāṭakavilakkiyamum, kumarināṭṭiliruntami aṟiyappaṭum.
kaṇakku
ai am pal eṉa varūum iṟuti
alpeyar eṇṇiṉum āyiyal niliyum	(tol. puḷḷi. 98)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil kuvaḷi, tāmari, caṅkam, veḷḷam, āmpal eṉṉum māpēreṇkaḷ kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
caṅkam = ilakkaṅ kōṭi
tāmari = kōṭā kōṭi
iraṇṭumuta loṉpā ṉiṟuti muṉṉar
 vaḻaṅkiyaṉ māveṉ kiḷavi tōṉṟiṉ	(tol. kuṟṟiya. 76)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā mā eṉṉum aḷavik kuṟippatāl, kīḻvāyilakkattic cērnta ēṉiyaḷavukaḷum tolkāppiyar kālattiṟku muṉpu vaḻaṅkiṉa veṉṟē koḷḷappaṭum.
kaṇiyam
tiṅkaḷu nāḷu muntukiḷan taṉṉa. 	(tol. uyir. 84)
ñāyiṟu tiṅkaḷ colleṉa varūum.	(tol. kiḷavi. 58)
maṟinta voḻukkattu ōriyum nāḷum.         (tol. kaḷa. 44)
eṉṉun tolkāppiya aṭikaḷ kaṇiyak kuṟippik koṇṭaṉa.
maruttuvam
potiya maliyil vatinta akattiyar oru maruttuva nūlāciriyar eṉṟu collappaṭuvataṉālum, peṟṟiya (citta) maruttuvam toṉṟutoṭṭu vaḻaṅkivarum tamiḻa maruttuva mātalālum, orucila maruntup peyarkaḷ akattiyar peyari aṭiyākap peṟṟiruppa taṉālum,
vāyuṟi vāḻttē vayaṅka nāṭiṉ
 vēmpuṅ kaṭuvum pōla veñcol 
 tāṅkuta liṉṟi vaḻinaṉi payakkumeṉ
 ṟōmpaṭik kiḷaviyiṉ vāyuṟut taṟṟē.      (tol. cey.111)
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāviluḷḷa maruttuvak kuṟip piṉālum, kumarināṭṭil maruttuva ilakkiya miruntami uyttuṇarappaṭum.
moḻinūl
centamiḻ koṭuntamiḻ eṉṉum moḻip pakuppum iyaṟcol  tiricol eṉṉum centamiḻc coṟpakuppum,
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
 taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticiccoṟ kiḷavi.	(tol. ecca. 4)
eṉṉum koṭuntamiḻc col viḷakkamum valittal melit tal virittal tokuttal nīṭṭal kuṟukkal eṉṉum coṟṟirivu muṟikaḷum, moḻinūliṉ karuniliyēyāyi ṉum, ulakil mutaṉmutaṟ collappaṭṭataṉāl, moḻi nūliṉ toṭakka mēyām. 
uḷanūl
eḷḷal iḷami pētimi maṭaṉeṉ
 ṟuḷḷap paṭṭa nakināṉ keṉpa.	(tol. meyp. 4)
iḷivē iḻavē acivē vaṟumiyeṉa
 viḷivil koḷki aḻuki nāṉkē.	 (tol. meyp. 5)
mūppē piṇiyē varuttam meṉmiyōṭu
 yāppuṟa vanta iḷivaral nāṉkē.	 (tol. meyp. 6)
putumi perumi ciṟumi ākkamoṭu
 matimi cālā maruṭki nāṉkē.	          (tol. meyp. 7)
aṇaṅkē  vilaṅkē kaḷvartam iṟiyeṉap
 piṇaṅkal cālā accam nāṉkē.	 (tol. meyp. 8)
kalvi taṟukaṇ icimi koṭiyeṉac
 collap paṭṭa perumitam nāṉkē.	 (tol. meyp. 9)
uṟuppaṟi kuṭikōḷ alikoli yeṉṟa
 veṟuppa vanta vekuḷi nāṉkē.	      (tol. meyp. 10)
celvam pulaṉē puṇarvuviḷi yāṭṭeṉ
 ṟallal nītta uvaki nāṉkē.	        (tol. meyp. 11)
iviyum piṟa meyppāṭṭiyal nūṟpākkaḷum uḷanūṟ karuvām ēraṇam (Logic).
akattiyat tarukka cūttiram
 1.	poruḷkuṇaṅ karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumi
	 iṉmi yuṭaṉporuḷ ēḻeṉa moḻipa.
 2.	maṇpuṉal aṉalkāl veḷipoḻu tāci
	 āṉmā maṉattō ṭoṉpatum poruḷē.
 3.	vaṭivam cuviyiru nāṟṟam ūṟeṉ
	 aḷavu vēṟṟumi puṇarcci pirivu
	 muṉmi piṉmi tiṇmi nekiḻcci
	 cikkeṉal ōci uṇarcci yiṉpam
	 tuṉpam viruppam veṟuppu muyaṟci
	 aṟamaṟam vātaṉiyoṭu kuṇamaṟu nāṉkē.
 4.	eḻumpal viḻutal vaḷital nimirtal
	 naṭatta luṭaṉē karumami vakittē.
 5.	potumi mēlkīḻ eṉa iru vakiyē.
 6.	maṉṉiya poruḷiṉ kaṇṇavā yavaṟṟiṉ
	 vēṟṟumi terippaṉa palavām ciṟappē.
 7.	oṟṟumi yāppaḵ toṉṟē yeṉka.
 8.	muṉṉiṉmi piṉṉiṉmi muṟṟu miṉmi
	 oṉṟiṉoṉ ṟiṉmiyeṉ ṟiṉmi nāṉkē.
 9.	maṇnīr aṉalkāl muṟiyē nāṟṟam
	 taṭpam veppam ūṟṟam āki
	 meypporuḷ aḻiporuḷ mēvum eṉka.
10.	aṇukkaḷ meypporuḷ kāriyam aḻiporuḷ
	 pirutivi nittiya anittiya vaṇampeṟum
	 niliyaṇup poruḷnili yillatu kāriyam.
11.	atuvē uṭalpoṟi viṭayam mūvakip paṭumē
	 nammaṉōr yākki maṇkūṟ ṟuṭampu
	 nāṟṟaṅ kavarvatu nāciyiṉ nuṉiyē
	 maṇkal mutaliya viṭayam ākum.
12.	nīriṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭunī ruṭampu
	 cuvittiṟam kavarvatu nāviṉ nuṉiyē
	 kaṭalyā ṟāti viṭayam ākum.
13.	katirōṉ varippiṟ kaṭṭaṉa luṭampē
      	 uruvam kavarvatu karumaṇi viḻiyē
      	 maṇviṇ vayiṟā karamnāl viṭayam.
14.	vaḷiyiṟi varippiṟ kaṭṭukā luṭampē
	 ūṟṟam kavarvatu mīntōl eṉka
	 viṭayam maramutal acitaṟ kētuvē
	 pirāṇaṉ uṭalakat tiyaṅkum kāṟṟē.
15.	vicumpē kālam ticiyō ṭāṉmā
	 maṉamivi yintum nittiyap poruḷē.
16.	ōcip paṇpiṟ ṟākā yammē.
17.	iṟappumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētuk kālam.
18.	kiḻakkumutal vaḻakkiṟ kētut ticiyē.
19.	aṟivup paṇpiṟ ṟāṉmā eṉka
	 iṟiyē īcaṉmuṟ ṟaṟivaṉōr mutalē
	 uyirtāṉ uṭaltoṟum vevvē ṟākum.
20.	maṉamaṇu vaṭivāy varumiṉ pāti
	 aṟitaṟ kiṉṟi yamiyāk karuvi
	 yākip palavāy aḻiviṉ ṟuṟumē.
ivi tamiḻp paṇṭitar kō. vaṭivēluc ceṭṭiyāriṉ tarukka paripāṣip patippiṟ kaṇṭavi. ēraṇam eṉpatu tarukka nūlik   kuṟikkum tūya teṉcol. ic col camaṟ kirutattil iṉmi kavaṉikkat takkatu.
ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar eṉṉum tirucciṟṟam palak kōvic cāttuppāt toṭarum,
 ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icikaṇak kiratañ cālam
  tāraṇam maṟamē cantam tampamnīr nilamu lōkam
  māraṇam poruḷeṉ ṟiṉṉa māṉanūl yāvum vāri
  vāraṇam koṇṭa tantō vaḻivaḻip peyarum māḷa
eṉṉum taṉippāṭalum, paṇṭit tamiḻilakkiyattil tarukkanūl  iruntamiyiyum, ataṉ peyar ēraṇam eṉpataṉiyum,  teḷivākak kāṭṭum.
tamiḻiluḷḷa ēraṇanūl orē vakiyē. vaṭamoḻi yiluḷḷa tarukka nūl vicēṭikam, niyāyikam eṉa iru vakippaṭum. avaṟṟi muṟiyē ciṟappikam, muṟiyikam eṉalām. tamiḻ ēraṇam ciṟappika muṟippaṭṭatākum. uṟaḻum allatu tarukkumvaki potuvāyiṉum, aṭippaṭip poruṭ pākupāṭṭi nōkkiṉ,  ciṟappika muṟiyē ciṟanta tum, muntiyatumākum.  ellāp poruḷkaḷiyum, muṟiyika muṟippaṭi patiṉāṟu vakiyākap pakuppatiṉum, ciṟappika muṟippaṭi eḻuvakiyākap pakuppatē muḻuniṟivāyum iṭiveḷiyillatāyum oṉṟiyoṉṟu taḻuvātatāyu miruttal kāṇka.
ēraṇam (Logic) eṉṉum col maṭṭumaṉṟit tarukkam (Debate) eṉṉuñ collum,  tamiḻāyiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. ērtal = eḻutal. ēraṇam = coṟpōril oruvaṉ taṉ pakivaṉmītu eḻucci koḷvataṟku ētuvāṉa muṟikaḷik kaṟpikkum nūl.
tuḷ-tuḷir. tuḷirttal = 1. koḻuntuviṭutal, 
2. ceḻittal.
tuḷir-taḷir. taḷirttal = 1. koḻuntu viṭutal, 
2. taḻittal.
māriyāl vaṟṟi niṉṟa cantaṉan taḷirtta tēpōl.	(cīvaka. 545)
3. ceḻittal.
tuḷ-taḷ - taḷa. taḷataḷattal = parumiyātal, koḻu koḻuttal.
taḷ-taḻu-taḻi. taḻittal = 1. ceḻittal, 2. mikutal. mittaḻiyā niṉṟa māmiṭaṟ ṟampalavaṉ (tirukkōvi, 102), 3. vaḷartal. meytaḻi kaṟpi (tiruviḷi. vaḷiya. 15).
taḻu- taḻukku. taḻukkutal = ceḻippuṟutal.
taḻukkiya nāḷil tarumamuñ ceyyīr	(tiruman. 254).
taḻukku - tarukku. tarukkutal = 1. mikuttal. taṉṉoṭu mavaḷoṭun tarukkiya puṇarttu. (tol. akat. 50), 2. ūkkam mikuttal. vempōrt tarukkiṉār mintar. (cīvaka. 1679), 
3.  akaṅkarittal. taṉṉi viyantu tarukkalum (tirikaṭu. 38).
tarukku - tarukkam = taṉ kūṟṟi mikukkum coṟpōr. tarukku - cerukku = akaṅkāram.
tarukku - va. tark. tarukkam - va. tarkka. tarukku eṉṉum coṟku vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamiṉmi kavaṉikkat takkatu.
mēṟkāṭṭiya ēraṇa nūṟpākkaḷ akattiyat tarukka nūṟpākkaḷallāviṭiṉum, tarukka nūli vaḷarttaṟkēṟṟa aṭippaṭi niṟivāyamintiruttal kāṇka. avi mikap paḻiyaṉavāyiruttal paṟṟiyē akattiyar peyaril vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa.
avaṟṟil varum vaṭacoṟkaḷ mikac cilavāyiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.

9. tamiḻar paraval
makkaṭ perukkam, vāṇikam, putunāṭu kāṇum viruppam, pōr, koḷḷi, paki, pañcam, kaṭalkōḷ mutaliya  pala karaṇiyaṅkaḷpaṟṟi, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻar palvēṟu nilikaḷiṟ palvēṟu ticiyiṟ paravic ceṉṟaṉar.
tamiḻ vaḷarcciyaṭintapiṉ avar paravic ceṉṟatu vaṭakku nōkkiyē. vaṅkat talinakarkku iṉṟum kāḷik kōṭṭam eṉṉum tūya teṉcoṟ peyar vaḻaṅkuvatē, paṇṭik kālattē tamiḻar paṉimalivari allatu kaṅkivari paraviyiruntaṉar eṉpataṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām.
tamiḻ iyaṉmoḻiyāyum orē moḻiyāyum vaḻaṅkiya taṉāl nīṇṭa kālamāka ataṟkuc ciṟappup peyar ēṟpaṭa villi; moḻiyeṉṉum potuppeyarē vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. vaṭakkē cellac cella moḻi mella mellat tirintatu. ataṉāl vaṭanāṭṭi moḻipeyar tēyam eṉṟaṉar. piṉṉart teṉṉāṭṭu moḻikkut tamiḻ eṉṉum peyar tōṉṟiṟṟu.
tamiḻ tirintu tamiḻarkku viḷaṅkāk kiḷimoḻiyāy māṟiyapōtu, tamiḻam eṉṉum collum tiraviṭam eṉṟu tirintatu. (tamiḻ - tamiḻam - tramiḷa - tramiṭa - traviṭa - tiraviṭam - tirāviṭam.) ataṉāl, tamiḻiṉiṉṟu tirinta moḻi tirāviṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. atu mutaṟkaṇ oṉṟāyiruntu piṉpu palavākak kiḷittatu. mutaṉ mutalākat tirinta tiraviṭamoḻi teluṅkātalāl atu vaṭaku (vaṭanāṭṭu allatu vaṭakkattu moḻi) eṉappaṭṭatu. atu piṉṉar vaṭuku eṉat tirintatu. vaṭuku eṉpatu ammīṟu peṟṟu vaṭukam eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. vaṭaku eṉpatu iṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattiṟ paṭaku eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. nīlamaliyil vāḻum kaṉṉaṭat tirimoḻiyāḷar paṭakar eṉappaṭutal kāṇka.
aṇmiyiṟ pirinta maliyāḷam nīṅkalākat tiraviṭa moḻikaḷellām vaṭapālē vaḻaṅkuvatum, vaṭakkē cellac cellat tirintum citintum ciṟuttum citaṟiyum ilakkaṇa vilakkiyam kuṉṟiyum iṉṟiyum pōvatum, intiyāviṟku veḷiyē vaḻaṅkāmiyum, iṉṟu tamiḻum ōraḷavu tirintuḷḷamiyum, kumarināṭṭut tamiḻē iṟṟit tamiḻukkum ellāt tiraviṭa moḻikaṭkum tāyeṉpatit terivikkum.
moḻikaḷiṉ allatu coṟkaḷiṉ iyalpum tiripum nōkkātu, ciṟitum peritum vaṭivoppumi yoṉṟiyē nōkki, tamiḻariṉ allatu tiraviṭariṉ muṉṉōr vaṭa mēli nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantirukkalāmeṉṟu, varalāṟṟāciriyar maṭṭumaṉṟi moḻi nūlaṟiñarum mayaṅki iṭarppaṭukiṉṟaṉar. coṟkaḷiṉ vērum varalāṟum aṟiyiṉ aṅṅaṉam iṭarp paṭār. eṭuttuk kāṭṭāka, oru teluṅkuc colliyum oru pirākuvīc colliyum eṭuttu īṇṭu viḷakkuvām.
teluṅkuc col : māṭṭāṭu,  māṭlāṭu.  māṟu-māṟṟam   = māṟiccollum  col,  col. māṟṟam - te. māṭṭa.        māṭṭa + āṭu = māṭṭāṭu. o. nō : collāṭu, uriyāṭu. 
te. māṭṭalu (paṉmi) + āṭu = māṭṭalāṭu. ta. kaḷ (paṉmiyīṟu)- te. kaḷu-kalu-lu. kaḷḷutal = kalattal, kūṭutal. kaḷ-kaḷam = kūṭṭam, avi. kūṭutalik kuṟikkum col, pala poruṭ kūṭṭamākiya paṉmiyiyuṇarttuṅ kuṟiyāyiṟṟu.
te. māṭṭu-ka. māttu, māttaṉu. māttu + āṭu = māttāṭu, māttaṉu + āṭu = māttaṉāṭu.

ātam (ātām) eṉṉum peyar, ceṅkaḷi maṇṇil  uruvākkappaṭṭavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ attam eṉṉum tamiḻccolloṭum, makkaḷiṉat tanti eṉṟu poruḷ paṭiṉ attaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻccolloṭum, (mutal) māntaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ ātaṉ (āṉmā) eṉṉum tamiḻc colloṭum, toṭarpuṭiyatā yirukkalām.

ēvā (ēvāḷ) eṉṉum peyar, makkaḷiṉattiṟku uyirit tantavaḷ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ āvi eṉṉum tamiḻc collōṭum, makkaḷiṉat tāy eṉṟu poruḷpaṭiṉ avvi eṉṉum tamiḻc collōṭum toṭarpuṭiyatā yirukkalām.
cumēriya nākarikatti vaḷartta makkaḷ vaḻiyiṉaruḷ oru cārār ekipatu vaḻiyākavum maṟṟoru cārār ciṉṉa āciyā vaḻiyākavum, naṇṇilakkaṭal pukuntu airōppāvi yaṭintu mēliyāriyarāka māṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
pūṉi ekipatu nāṭṭil pala nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷāka vaḷarkkap paṭṭu vantapiṉ ki.pi. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭil airōppāviṟkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭateṉṟum, atu kirēkkattil katta eṉṟum ilattīṉil kattus eṉṟum peyar peṟṟateṉṟum, āriya nāṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ pirintu pōkumuṉ avaṟṟiṭi ataṟkup potup peyar illiyeṉṟum, camaṟkirutattil. mārkāra (margara) viṭāla eṉṉum iru peyar ataṟkuṇ ṭeṉṟum, mākkacu mullar kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār.
pūṉi toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻakak kāṭṭil iyaṟki yāka vāḻntu varukiṉṟatu. anniliyil ataṟku veruku eṉṟu peyar. atu vīṭṭiṟ paḻakkappaṭṭa piṉ, aṭikkaṭi mukam pūcuvatāl (kaḻuvuvatāl) pūciyeṉṟum, piḷḷipōl vaḷarkkappaṭṭamiyāl piḷḷi yeṉṟum, pakaṟkālattil naṉṟāyk kaṇ teriyāmiyāl kotti yeṉṟum, peyar peṟṟatu.

(f. It. and Sp.) = a promenade or esplanade by the sea.
paṭaku paṭam = cīli, tiriccīli, pāyccīli. paṭam - paṭavu - paṭaku. paṭavu- paṭavar - paravar. cempaṭavu - cempaṭavar.
